\displaystyle\ {\mathbb{E}}\left[\left|\left\{\bar{{\mathsf{z}}}_{k_{x}}(X_{1})\otimes\int_{{\mathcal{T}}}\bar{{\mathsf{w}}}_{k_{t}}(t)\mathsf{p}_{T}(t){\mathrm{d}}t\right\}^{\top}\Sigma_{k}^{-1}\bar{\phi}_{k}(X_{2},T_{2})\right|^{2}v^{\top}\left\{\widehat{b}_{\bm{\beta}}(X_{2},T_{2})-\Gamma(Y_{2},T_{2},\bm{\beta})\right\}^{\otimes 2}v\right]

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\ {\mathbb{E}}\left[\left\{\bar{{\mathsf{z}}}_{k_{x}}(X_{1})\otimes\int_{{\mathcal{T}}}\bar{{\mathsf{w}}}_{k_{t}}(t)\mathsf{p}_{T}(t){\mathrm{d}}t\right\}^{\top}\Sigma_{k}^{-1}\left\{\bar{{\mathsf{z}}}_{k_{x}}(X_{1})\otimes\int_{{\mathcal{T}}}\bar{{\mathsf{w}}}_{k_{t}}(t)\mathsf{p}_{T}(t){\mathrm{d}}t\right\}\right]

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\ \lambda_{\max}(\Sigma_{k}^{-1}){\mathbb{E}}\left[\left(\bar{{\mathsf{z}}}_{k_{x}}(X_{i_{1}})\otimes\int_{{\mathcal{T}}}\bar{{\mathsf{w}}}_{k_{t}}(t)\mathsf{p}_{T}(t){\mathrm{d}}t\right)^{\top}\left(\bar{{\mathsf{z}}}_{k_{x}}(X_{i_{1}})\otimes\int_{{\mathcal{T}}}\bar{{\mathsf{w}}}_{k_{t}}(t)\mathsf{p}_{T}(t){\mathrm{d}}t\right)\right]

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\ \lambda_{\max}(\Sigma_{k}^{-1}){\mathbb{E}}\left[\bar{{\mathsf{z}}}_{k_{x}}(X)^{\top}\bar{{\mathsf{z}}}_{k_{x}}(X)\right]{\mathbb{E}}\left[\bar{{\mathsf{w}}}_{k_{t}}(T)^{\top}\bar{{\mathsf{w}}}_{k_{t}}(T)\right]\lesssim k_{x}k_{t}=k.

where
\lambda_{\max}(\Sigma_{k}^{-1})
is the largest eigenvalue of matrix
\Sigma_{k}^{-1}
. Similarly, we derive

\displaystyle\ {\mathbb{E}}\left[\left|\bar{\phi}_{k}(X_{1},T_{1})^{\top}\Sigma_{k}^{-1}\bar{\phi}_{k}(X_{2},T_{2})\right|^{2}v^{\top}\left\{\widehat{b}_{\bm{\beta}}(X_{2},T_{2})-\Gamma(Y_{2},T_{2},\bm{\beta})\right\}^{\otimes 2}v\right]

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\ {\mathbb{E}}\left[\bar{\phi}_{k}(X_{1},T_{1})^{\top}\Sigma_{k}^{-1}\bar{\phi}_{k}(X_{1},T_{1})\right]

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\ \lambda_{\max}(\Sigma_{k}^{-1}){\mathbb{E}}\left[\bar{\phi}_{k}(X,T)^{\top}\bar{\phi}_{k}(X,T)\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\ \lambda_{\max}(\Sigma_{k}^{-1})\iint_{{\mathcal{X}}\times{\mathcal{T}}}\xi(x,t)^{-1}\left(\bar{{\mathsf{z}}}_{k_{x}}(x)^{\top}\bar{{\mathsf{z}}}_{k_{x}}(x)\right)\otimes\left(\bar{{\mathsf{w}}}_{k_{t}}(t)^{\top}\bar{{\mathsf{w}}}_{k_{t}}(t)\right)\mathsf{p}_{T}(t)\mathsf{p}_{X}(x){\mathrm{d}}t{\mathrm{d}}x

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\ \lambda_{\max}(\Sigma_{k}^{-1}){\mathbb{E}}\left[\bar{{\mathsf{z}}}_{k_{x}}(X)^{\top}\bar{{\mathsf{z}}}_{k_{x}}(X)\right]{\mathbb{E}}\left[\bar{{\mathsf{w}}}_{k_{t}}(T)^{\top}\bar{{\mathsf{w}}}_{k_{t}}(T)\right]\lesssim k.

Thus

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left|{\mathbb{E}}[\mathsf{IF}_{\mathrm{B}_{\psi,k}}^{(2)}(O_{1},O_{2},O_{3};\bm{\beta})^{\top}v\mid O_{1},O_{2}]\right|^{2}\right]=O(k).

Similarly, we have

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left|{\mathbb{E}}[\mathsf{IF}_{\mathrm{B}_{\psi,k}}^{(2)}(O_{1},O_{2},O_{3};\bm{\beta})^{\top}v\mid O_{1},O_{3}]\right|^{2}\right]=O(k),

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left|{\mathbb{E}}[\mathsf{IF}_{\mathrm{B}_{\psi,k}}^{(2)}(O_{1},O_{2},O_{3};\bm{\beta})^{\top}v\mid O_{2},O_{3}]\right|^{2}\right]=O(k).

Hence,

\displaystyle\zeta_{2}\lesssim\frac{k}{n^{2}}.

For
\zeta_{3}
, we have

\displaystyle\ \zeta_{3}\lesssim\frac{1}{n^{3}}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left(\mathsf{IF}_{\mathrm{B}_{\psi,k}}^{(2)}(O_{1},O_{2},O_{3};\bm{\beta})^{\top}v\right)^{2}\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\ \frac{1}{n^{3}}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left|\left\{\widehat{\xi}(X_{1},T_{1})\bar{\phi}_{k}(X_{1},T_{1})-\bar{{\mathsf{z}}}_{k_{x}}(X_{1})\otimes\bar{{\mathsf{w}}}_{k_{t}}(T_{3})\right\}^{\top}\Sigma_{k}^{-1}\bar{\phi}_{k}(X_{2},T_{2})\right|^{2}\right.

\displaystyle\qquad\times\left.v^{\top}\left\{\widehat{b}_{\bm{\beta}}(X_{2},T_{2})-\Gamma(Y_{2},T_{2},\bm{\beta})\right\}^{\otimes 2}v\right]

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\ \frac{1}{n^{3}}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left\{\widehat{\xi}(X,T)-\xi(X,T)\right\}^{2}\bar{\phi}_{k}(X,T)^{\top}\Sigma_{k}^{-1}\bar{\phi}_{k}(X,T)\right]

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\ \frac{1}{n^{3}}\lambda_{max}(\Sigma_{k}^{-1}){\mathbb{E}}\left[\bar{\phi}_{k}(X,T)^{\top}\bar{\phi}_{k}(X,T)\right]\lesssim\frac{k}{n^{3}}.

Combining the above analyses, we complete the proof.
∎

Our next result characterizes the asymptotic distribution of
\widetilde{\psi}_{k}^{(2)}(\bm{\beta}^{\ast})
(defined in Section
S7
). Before stating the lemma, recall the definitions in (
S7.12
) and (
S7.13
):

\displaystyle V_{1}(\bm{\beta}^{\ast})

\displaystyle\coloneqq\lim\limits_{n\to\infty}\frac{n}{n+k}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left\{2\pi_{1}\widehat{\Xi}_{\bm{\beta}^{\ast}}+3\pi_{1}\mathsf{IF}_{\mathrm{B}_{\psi,k}}^{(2)}(\bm{\beta}^{*})\right\}^{\otimes 2}\right]

\displaystyle V_{2}(\bm{\beta}^{*})

\displaystyle\coloneqq\lim\limits_{n\to\infty}\frac{1}{k+n}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left\{\pi_{2}\widehat{\Xi}_{\bm{\beta}^{\ast}}+3\pi_{2}\mathsf{IF}_{\mathrm{B}_{\psi,k}}^{(2)}(\bm{\beta}^{*})\right\}^{\otimes 2}\right].

Lemma 16
.

Conditioning on the nuisance sample, suppose that Assumptions
6
–
8
hold.

If
k\ll n
,

\displaystyle\sqrt{n}\left\{\widetilde{\psi}_{k}^{(2)}(\bm{\beta}^{\ast})-\bar{\psi}_{k}(\bm{\beta}^{\ast})\right\}=

\displaystyle\ \sqrt{n}\cdot{\mathbb{U}}_{n,1}\left(2\pi_{1}\widehat{\Xi}_{\bm{\beta}^{\ast}}+3\pi_{1}\mathsf{IF}_{\mathrm{B}_{\psi,k}}^{(2)}(\bm{\beta}^{*})\right)+o_{\mathbb{P}}(1)\stackrel{{\scriptstyle d}}{{\to}}\ N(0,V_{1}(\bm{\beta}^{\ast})).

If
k\gg n
,

\displaystyle\sqrt{\frac{n^{2}}{k}}\left\{\widetilde{\psi}_{k}^{(2)}(\bm{\beta}^{\ast})-\bar{\psi}_{k}(\bm{\beta}^{\ast})\right\}=\sqrt{\frac{n^{2}}{k}}\cdot{\mathbb{U}}_{n,2}\left(\pi_{2}\widehat{\Xi}_{\bm{\beta}^{\ast}}+3\pi_{2}\mathsf{IF}_{\mathrm{B}_{\psi,k}}^{(2)}(\bm{\beta}^{*})\right)+o_{\mathbb{P}}(1)\stackrel{{\scriptstyle d}}{{\to}}N(0,V_{2}(\bm{\beta}^{\ast})/2).

If
k/n\rightarrow\tau
for some
\tau\in(0,\infty)
,

\displaystyle\sqrt{n}\left\{\widetilde{\psi}_{k}^{(2)}(\bm{\beta}^{\ast})-\bar{\psi}_{k}(\bm{\beta}^{\ast})\right\}

\displaystyle=\sqrt{n}\cdot{\mathbb{U}}_{n,1}\left(2\pi_{1}\widehat{\Xi}_{\bm{\beta}^{\ast}}+3\pi_{1}\mathsf{IF}_{\mathrm{B}_{\psi,k}}^{(2)}(\bm{\beta}^{*})\right)

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad+\sqrt{n}\cdot{\mathbb{U}}_{n,2}\left(\pi_{2}\widehat{\Xi}_{\bm{\beta}^{\ast}}+3\pi_{2}\mathsf{IF}_{\mathrm{B}_{\psi,k}}^{(2)}(\bm{\beta}^{*})\right)+o_{\mathbb{P}}(1)

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle d}}{{\to}}N\left(0,(1+\tau)\left\{V_{1}(\bm{\beta}^{\ast})+\frac{1}{2}V_{2}(\bm{\beta}^{\ast})\right\}\right).

Here,
\Pi^{\perp}[f\mid\bar{\phi}_{k}]
denotes the orthocomplement of the projection of
f
onto the linear span of
\bar{\phi}_{k}
, as defined in Section
1.3
.
\widetilde{\psi}_{k}^{(2)}(\bm{\beta}^{\ast})
and
\bar{\psi}_{k}(\bm{\beta}^{\ast})
are defined in Section
S7
.

Remark 15
.

Since this lemma is established conditional on the nuisance sample, and both
\widehat{\Xi}_{\bm{\beta}^{*}}
and
\mathsf{IF}_{\mathrm{B}_{\psi,k}}^{(2)}
depend on the initial estimator
\bm{\beta}_{\mathsf{init}}
, the asymptotic distributions of
\widetilde{\psi}_{k}^{(2)}(\bm{\beta}^{\ast})-\bar{\psi}_{k}(\bm{\beta}^{\ast})
established in this lemma also implicitly depend on
\bm{\beta}_{\mathsf{init}}
.
∎

Remark 16
.

In Assumptions
3
and
7
, we have assumed that the observations are bounded. For the CLT established in Lemma
16
, the boundedness assumption can be relaxed if one uses Corollary 1.4 in
Bhattacharya and Ghosh 1992
instead of their Corollary 1.5. The former only requires certain moment conditions to be satisfied, avoiding making the stronger boundedness assumption. We decide to stick to the boundedness assumption, following the related literature on HOIFs
(
15
;
20
)
.
∎

Proof.

Take a unit-norm vector
v\in{\mathbb{R}}^{q}
(
\|v\|=1
) and define

\displaystyle g_{n}(O_{1})\coloneqq\frac{a^{\top}\left\{2\pi_{1}\widehat{\Xi}_{\bm{\beta}^{\ast}}+3\pi_{1}\mathsf{IF}_{\mathrm{B}_{\psi,k}}^{(2)}(\bm{\beta}^{*})\right\}}{\sqrt{k+n}},

\displaystyle h_{n}(O_{1},O_{2})\coloneqq\frac{a^{\top}\left\{\pi_{2}\widehat{\Xi}_{\bm{\beta}^{\ast}}+3\pi_{2}\mathsf{IF}_{\mathrm{B}_{\psi,k}}^{(2)}(\bm{\beta}^{*})\right\}}{n\sqrt{k+n}}.

Then,
{\mathbb{E}}[g_{n}(O_{1})]=0
, and
h_{n}(O_{1},O_{2})
is degenerate in view of the definition of
\pi_{2}
given in (
S6.2
) in Section
S6.1
, and

\displaystyle\lim\limits_{n\to\infty}n^{2}{\mathbb{E}}[h_{n}(O_{1},O_{2})h_{n}(O_{1},O_{2})^{\top}]=a^{\top}V_{2}(\bm{\beta}^{\ast})a,

\displaystyle\lim\limits_{n\to\infty}n{\mathbb{E}}[g_{n}(O_{1})g_{n}(O_{1})^{\top}]=a^{\top}V_{1}(\bm{\beta}^{\ast})a.

By Corollary 1.5 in
Bhattacharya and Ghosh 1992
, if the following conditions hold

\displaystyle\sup_{o}|g_{n}(o)|\to 0,

(S7.24a)

\displaystyle\sup_{o_{1},o_{2}}|h_{n}(o_{1},o_{2})|\to 0,

(S7.24b)

\displaystyle n\sup_{o_{1}}{\mathbb{E}}|h_{n}(o_{1},O_{2})|\to 0,

(S7.24c)

then we obtain the following central limit theorem jointly for
\sum_{j}g_{n}(O_{j})
and
\sum_{1\leq i_{1}\neq i_{2}\leq n}h_{n}(O_{i_{1}},O_{i_{2}})
:

\left(\begin{matrix}\sum_{j}g_{n}(O_{j})\\
\sum_{1\leq i_{1}\neq i_{2}\leq n}h_{n}(O_{i_{1}},O_{i_{2}})\end{matrix}\right)\stackrel{{\scriptstyle d}}{{\to}}N\left(\left(\begin{matrix}0\\
0\end{matrix}\right),\left[\begin{matrix}a^{\top}V_{1}(\bm{\beta}^{\ast})a&0\\
0&\frac{1}{2}a^{\top}V_{2}(\bm{\beta}^{\ast})a\end{matrix}\right]\right).

By the Hoeffding decomposition (recall from Section
S6.1
), we have

\displaystyle\widetilde{\psi}_{k}^{(2)}(\bm{\beta}^{\ast})-\bar{\psi}_{k}(\bm{\beta}^{\ast})=

\displaystyle\ {\mathbb{U}}_{n,3}\pi_{3}\mathsf{IF}_{\mathrm{B}_{\psi,k}}^{(2)}(\bm{\beta}^{*})+3{\mathbb{U}}_{n,2}\pi_{2}\mathsf{IF}_{\mathrm{B}_{\psi,k}}^{(2)}(\bm{\beta}^{*})

\displaystyle+3{\mathbb{U}}_{n,1}\pi_{1}\mathsf{IF}_{\mathrm{B}_{\psi,k}}^{(2)}(\bm{\beta}^{*})+{\mathbb{U}}_{n,2}\pi_{2}\widehat{\Xi}_{\bm{\beta}^{\ast}}+2{\mathbb{U}}_{n,1}\pi_{1}\widehat{\Xi}_{\bm{\beta}^{\ast}}.

Then, in view of
{n}/{\sqrt{k+n}}\cdot{\mathbb{U}}_{n,3}\pi_{3}\mathsf{IF}_{\mathrm{B}_{\psi,k}}^{(2)}(\bm{\beta}^{*})=o_{\mathbb{P}}(1)
, we have

\displaystyle\frac{n}{\sqrt{k+n}}\cdot a^{\top}\left\{\widetilde{\psi}_{k}^{(2)}(\bm{\beta}^{\ast})-\bar{\psi}_{k}(\bm{\beta}^{\ast})\right\}=\sum_{1\leq i_{1}\neq i_{2}\leq n}h_{n}(O_{i_{1}},O_{i_{2}})+\sum_{j}g_{n}(O_{j})+o_{\mathbb{P}}(1).

If
k\ll n
, then
V_{2}=0
and
{n}/{\sqrt{k+n}}=\sqrt{n}+o(1)
, which implies that

\displaystyle\sqrt{n}\cdot a^{\top}\left\{\widetilde{\psi}_{k}^{(2)}(\bm{\beta}^{\ast})-\bar{\psi}_{k}(\bm{\beta}^{\ast})\right\}=

\displaystyle\sum_{j}g_{n}(O_{j})+o_{\mathbb{P}}(1)\stackrel{{\scriptstyle d}}{{\to}}N\left(0,a^{\top}V_{1}(\bm{\beta}^{\ast})a\right).

If
k\gg n
, then
V_{1}=0
and
{n}/{\sqrt{k+n}}=n/\sqrt{k}+o(1)
, which implies that

\displaystyle\frac{n}{\sqrt{k}}\cdot a^{\top}\left\{\widetilde{\psi}_{k}^{(2)}(\bm{\beta}^{\ast})-\bar{\psi}_{k}(\bm{\beta}^{\ast})\right\}=

\displaystyle\ \sum_{1\leq i_{1}\neq i_{2}\leq n}h_{n}(O_{i_{1}},O_{i_{2}})+o_{\mathbb{P}}(1)\stackrel{{\scriptstyle d}}{{\to}}N\left(0,a^{\top}V_{2}(\bm{\beta}^{\ast})a/2\right).

If
k=\tau n
for some constant
\tau
, then
{n}/{\sqrt{k+n}}=\sqrt{n}/\sqrt{1+\tau}
and

\displaystyle\sqrt{n}\cdot a^{\top}\left\{\widetilde{\psi}_{k}^{(2)}(\bm{\beta}^{\ast})-\bar{\psi}_{k}(\bm{\beta}^{\ast})\right\}=

\displaystyle\ \sqrt{1+\tau}\left\{\sum_{j}g_{n}(O_{j})+\sum_{1\leq i_{1}\neq i_{2}\leq n}h_{n}(O_{i_{1}},O_{i_{2}})\right\}+o_{\mathbb{P}}(1)

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle d}}{{\to}}

\displaystyle\ N\left(0,(1+\tau)a^{\top}\left\{V_{1}(\bm{\beta}^{\ast})+\frac{1}{2}V_{2}(\bm{\beta}^{\ast})\right\}a\right).

It remains to verify whether Conditions (
S7.24a
)–(
S7.24c
) hold.

Condition (
S7.24a
)
:
Using (
S7.9
), Condition (
S7.24a
) holds by Assumption
6
and Assumption
7
, and the result in (
S7.1
).

Condition (
S7.24b
)
:
By Assumption
6
and Assumption
7
, we have

\displaystyle\sup_{o_{1},o_{2}}\left|a^{\top}\pi_{2}\widehat{\Xi}_{\bm{\beta}^{\ast}}(o_{1},o_{2})\right|\leq\sup_{o_{1},o_{2}}\left|a^{\top}\widehat{\Xi}_{\bm{\beta}^{\ast}}(o_{1},o_{2})\right|\lesssim 1

and

\displaystyle\sup_{o_{1},o_{2}}\left|a^{\top}\pi_{2}\mathsf{IF}_{\mathrm{B}_{\psi,k}}^{(2)}(o_{1},o_{2};\bm{\beta}^{*})\right|

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\sup_{o_{1},o_{2},o_{3}}\left|a^{\top}\mathsf{IF}_{\mathrm{B}_{\psi,k}}^{(2)}(o_{1},o_{2},o_{3};\bm{\beta}^{*})\right|

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\ \left\|(\widehat{b}_{\bm{\beta}_{\mathsf{init}}}-\Gamma_{\bm{\beta}^{\ast}})(\cdot,\cdot)\right\|_{\infty}\cdot\sup_{o_{1},o_{2},o_{3}}\left|\left\{\widehat{\xi}(x_{1},t_{1})\bar{\phi}_{k}(x_{1},t_{1})-\bar{{\mathsf{z}}}_{k_{x}}(x_{1})\otimes\bar{{\mathsf{w}}}_{k_{t}}(t_{3})\right\}^{\top}\Sigma_{k}^{-1}\bar{\phi}_{k}(x_{2},t_{2})\right|

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\ \zeta(k)^{2},

where the last inequality holds by Assumption
8
. Thus we have

\displaystyle\sup_{o_{1},o_{2}}|h_{n}(o_{1},o_{2})|\lesssim\frac{\zeta(k)^{2}}{n\sqrt{k+n}}\to 0.

Condition (
S7.24c
)
:
Denote
\bar{\phi}_{k}\coloneqq\{\phi_{1},\cdots,\phi_{k}\}
. By Assumption
8
, there are at most
c_{8}
(
c_{8}
is a positive integer that does not depend on
n
) nonzero terms in the sum
\sum_{i=1}^{k}\left|\phi_{i}(x,t)\phi_{i}(x_{2},t_{2})\right|\cdot\left|a^{\top}\left\{\widehat{b}_{\bm{\beta}_{\mathsf{init}}}(x_{2},t_{2})-\Gamma(y_{2},t_{2},\bm{\beta}^{\ast})\right\}\right|
for any
(o,o_{2})\in{\mathcal{O}}^{2}
and all nonzero terms are bounded by
ζ
(k)^2 ‖ a^⊤ {
^

b

_
β
_
init
(⋅, ⋅) - Γ(⋅, ⋅,
β
^∗) } ‖_∞.
In addition, for any
i
, the probability of the set of
o_{2}\in{\mathcal{O}}
such that
|\phi_{i}(x,t)\phi_{i}(x_{2},t_{2})|\cdot|a^{\top}\{\widehat{b}_{\bm{\beta}_{\mathsf{init}}}(x_{2},t_{2})-\Gamma(y_{2},t_{2},\bm{\beta}^{\ast})\}|\neq 0
is bounded by
1/k
.
Then, for any
(x,t)\in\mathcal{X}\times\mathcal{T}
,

\displaystyle\ {\mathbb{E}}\left[\left|\bar{\phi}_{k}(x,t)^{\top}\Sigma_{k}^{-1}\bar{\phi}_{k}(X_{2},T_{2})a^{\top}\left\{\widehat{b}_{\bm{\beta}_{\mathsf{init}}}(X_{2},T_{2})-\Gamma(Y_{2},T_{2},\bm{\beta}^{*})\right\}\right|\right]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\ \lambda_{\max}\left(\Sigma_{k}^{-1}\right){\mathbb{E}}\left[\left|\bar{\phi}_{k}(x,t)^{\top}\bar{\phi}_{k}(X_{2},T_{2})\right|\cdot\left|a^{\top}\left\{\widehat{b}_{\bm{\beta}_{\mathsf{init}}}(X_{2},T_{2})-\Gamma(Y_{2},T_{2},\bm{\beta}^{*})\right\}\right|\right]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\ \lambda_{\max}\left(\Sigma_{k}^{-1}\right){\mathbb{E}}\left[\sum_{i=1}^{k}\left|\phi_{i}(x,t)\phi_{i}(X_{2},T_{2})\right|\cdot\left|a^{\top}\left\{\widehat{b}_{\bm{\beta}_{\mathsf{init}}}(X_{2},T_{2})-\Gamma(Y_{2},T_{2},\bm{\beta}^{*})\right\}\right|\right]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\ \frac{1}{k}\lambda_{\max}\left(\Sigma_{k}^{-1}\right)c_{8}\cdot\sup_{(o,o_{2})\in{\mathcal{O}}^{2}}|\phi_{i}(x,t)\phi_{i}(x_{2},t_{2})|\cdot\left|a^{\top}\left\{\widehat{b}_{\bm{\beta}_{\mathsf{init}}}(x_{2},t_{2})-\Gamma(y_{2},t_{2},\bm{\beta}^{*})\right\}\right|\cdot\|\mathsf{p}_{X,T}(\cdot,\cdot)\|_{\infty}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\ \frac{1}{k}\lambda_{\max}\left(\Sigma_{k}^{-1}\right)\zeta(k)^{2}\left\|a^{\top}(\widehat{b}_{\bm{\beta}_{\mathsf{init}}}-\Gamma_{\bm{\beta}^{\ast}})(\cdot,\cdot)\right\|_{\infty}=O(1),

where
\lambda_{\max}(\Sigma_{k}^{-1})
denotes the largest eigenvalue of
\Sigma_{k}^{-1}
and the last equality holds by Assumptions
6
–
8
. As a result of Assumption
6
and Jensen’s inequality,

\displaystyle\sup_{o}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left|\big\{\widehat{\xi}(x,t)\bar{\phi}_{k}(x,t)-\bar{{\mathsf{z}}}_{k_{x}}(x)\otimes\int_{{\mathcal{T}}}\bar{{\mathsf{w}}}_{k_{t}}(u)\mathsf{p}_{T}(u){\mathrm{d}}u\big\}^{\top}\Sigma_{k}^{-1}\bar{\phi}_{k}(X_{2},T_{2})a^{\top}\big\{\widehat{b}_{\bm{\beta}_{\mathsf{init}}}(X_{2},T_{2})-\Gamma(Y_{2},T_{2},\bm{\beta}^{*})\big\}\right\|\right]

\displaystyle\lesssim\sup_{o}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left|\left(\bar{{\mathsf{z}}}_{k_{x}}(x)\otimes\bar{{\mathsf{w}}}_{k_{t}}(t)\right)^{\top}\Sigma_{k}^{-1}\bar{\phi}_{k}(X_{2},T_{2})a^{\top}\left\{\widehat{b}_{\bm{\beta}_{\mathsf{init}}}(X_{2},T_{2})-\Gamma(Y_{2},T_{2},\bm{\beta}^{*})\right\}\right|\right]=O(1).

Similarly, analogous results can be obtained for the remaining terms in
\pi_{2}\mathsf{IF}_{\mathrm{B}_{\psi,k}}^{(2)}(o_{1},O_{2};\bm{\beta}^{*})
.
Taking these together, we have:

\displaystyle n\sup_{o_{1}}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left|\frac{a^{\top}\left\{\pi_{2}\mathsf{IF}_{\mathrm{B}_{\psi,k}}^{(2)}(o_{1},O_{2};\bm{\beta}^{*})\right\}}{n\sqrt{k+n}}\right|\right]\leq\frac{n}{n\sqrt{k+n}}\cdot O(1)=o(1).

Finally, since
\sup_{o_{1},o_{2}}\left|a^{\top}\pi_{2}\widehat{\Xi}_{\bm{\beta}^{\ast}}(o_{1},o_{2})\right|\lesssim 1
, we obtain the following:

\displaystyle n\sup_{o_{1}}{\mathbb{E}}\|h_{n}(o_{1},O_{2})\|\leq\frac{n}{n\sqrt{k+n}}\cdot O(1)=o(1).

∎

Appendix S8
Proof of Results in Section
4

To aid the exposition, we define the following quantities:

\displaystyle 1/\lambda(x)\coloneqq

\displaystyle\ \frac{\mathsf{p}_{X}(x)}{g(x)}=\int_{u\in{\mathcal{T}}}K_{t,h}(u)\mathsf{p}_{T|X}(u|x){\mathrm{d}}u,

(S8.1)

\displaystyle\Delta_{1}(x)\coloneqq

\displaystyle\int_{u\in{\mathcal{T}}}\{\widehat{\xi}(x,u)-\xi(x,u)-\widehat{\xi}(x,t)+\xi(x,t)\}K_{t,h}(u)\mathsf{p}_{T|X}(u|x){\mathrm{d}}u,

(S8.2)

\displaystyle\Delta_{2}(x)\coloneqq

\displaystyle\int_{u\in{\mathcal{T}}}\{b_{\beta}(x,u)-\widehat{b}_{\beta_{\mathsf{init}}}(x,u)-b_{\beta}(x,t)+\widehat{b}_{\beta_{\mathsf{init}}}(x,t)\}K_{t,h}(u)\mathsf{p}_{T|X}(u|x){\mathrm{d}}u.

(S8.3)

Using Assumption
1
(ii) and Assumption
15
, and a similar argument to that used in the proof of Theorem 1 in
(
12
)
, we have

\displaystyle 0<\underline{c}_{\lambda}\leq\lambda(x)\leq\bar{c}_{\lambda}<\infty\ \text{for any }x,\text{ and }\sup_{x\in{\mathcal{X}}}|\Delta_{j}(x)|\lesssim h^{\alpha_{1}\wedge\alpha_{2}}\text{, for }j=1,2,

(S8.4)

where
\underline{c}_{\lambda},\bar{c}_{\lambda}
are two fixed constants.
By the definitions of
\lambda(x)
and
g(x)
(defined in Section
4.1
), we have, for any function
h(x)
,

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}[h(X)]=\int_{x\in{\mathcal{X}}}h(x)\mathsf{p}_{X}(x){\mathrm{d}}x=\int_{x\in{\mathcal{X}}}h(x)\lambda(x)g(x){\mathrm{d}}x,

(S8.5)

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}[h(X)K_{t,h}(T)]=\int_{x\in{\mathcal{X}}}h(x)g(x){\mathrm{d}}x.

(S8.6)

By definition of
\lambda(x)
and (
S8.4
), we have

\displaystyle a^{\top}\widetilde{\Omega}_{k_{x}}\,a=a^{\top}\int_{x\in{\mathcal{X}}}g(x)\bar{{\mathsf{z}}}_{k_{x}}(x)\bar{{\mathsf{z}}}_{k_{x}}(x)^{\top}{\mathrm{d}}x\,a=a^{\top}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\lambda(X)\bar{{\mathsf{z}}}_{k_{x}}(X)^{\otimes 2}\right]\,a\asymp a^{\top}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\bar{{\mathsf{z}}}_{k_{x}}(X)^{\otimes 2}\right]\,a.

Hence, Assumption
8
(iii) implies that the eigenvalues of
\widetilde{\Omega}_{k_{x}}
are bounded from above and away from zero uniformly in
k_{x}
. Consequently, analogues to (
S7.1
), using Assumptions
8
, we have:
for any function
f\in{\mathcal{C}}({\mathcal{X}})
,

\displaystyle\left\|\Pi_{g}[f\mid\bar{{\mathsf{z}}}_{k_{x}}](\cdot)\right\|_{\infty}\leq\|f(\cdot)\|_{\infty},

(S8.7)

where
\Pi_{g}[f\mid\bar{{\mathsf{z}}}_{k_{x}}](\cdot)=\left(\int_{{\mathcal{X}}}f(x)\bar{{\mathsf{z}}}_{k_{x}}(x)g(x){\mathrm{d}}x\right)^{\top}\widetilde{\Omega}_{k_{x}}^{-1}\bar{{\mathsf{z}}}_{k_{x}}(\cdot)
.

Under Assumption
6
(iii), for any
t\in{\mathcal{T}}
and
\delta>0
, we have

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}\big[\sup_{|\beta_{1}-\beta_{2}|\leq\delta}\left|\Gamma(Y,T,\beta_{1})-\Gamma(Y,T,\beta_{2})\right|^{2}\mid T=t\big]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\ \frac{1}{c_{5}}{\mathbb{E}}\big[\sup_{|\beta_{1}-\beta_{2}|\leq\delta}\left|\Gamma(Y,T,\beta_{1})-\Gamma(Y,T,\beta_{2})\right|^{2}\mid T=t\big]\mathsf{p}_{T}(t)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\ \frac{1}{c_{5}}{\mathbb{E}}\big[\sup_{|\beta_{1}-\beta_{2}|\leq\delta}\left|\Gamma(Y,T,\beta_{1})-\Gamma(Y,T,\beta_{2})\right|^{2}\big].

Hence, applying Assumption
7
(iii), we obtain

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}^{1/2}\big[\sup_{|\beta_{1}-\beta_{2}|\leq\delta}\left|\Gamma(Y,T,\beta_{1})-\Gamma(Y,T,\beta_{2})\right|^{2}\mid T=t\big]\lesssim\delta^{\alpha_{0}}.

(S8.8)

S8.1
Proof of Theorem
6

Because the proof carefully tracks dependence on
\beta
, we now make that dependence explicit by moving
\beta
out of subscripts into argument parentheses.
Denote population counterpart of
\widetilde{\psi}_{t,k}^{(2)}(\beta)
as given by
\bar{\psi}_{t,k}(\beta)\coloneqq{\mathbb{E}}[\widetilde{\psi}_{t,k}^{(2)}(\beta)]
.

Proof of Theorem
6
.

Assumption
14
, together with an argument similar to that leading to
(
S7.2
) in Section
S7.1
, implies

\displaystyle|\psi_{t}(\beta)|\gtrsim|\beta-\beta^{*}_{t}|\wedge c

(S8.9)

for some constant
c>0
. Consequently, to bound

|\widetilde{\beta}^{(2)}_{t}-\beta^{\ast}_{t}|
,
it suffices to control the quantity

|\psi_{t}(\widetilde{\beta}^{(2)}_{t})|
.

First, we establish the consistency of
\widetilde{\beta}^{(2)}_{t}
by showing that
|\psi_{t}(\widetilde{\beta}^{(2)}_{t})|\lesssim\widetilde{r}_{n,\mathsf{np}}\rightarrow 0
with probability approaching one, where
\widetilde{r}_{n,\mathsf{np}}
is defined in Theorem
6
. Note that
\psi_{t}(\beta^{*}_{t})\equiv 0
and
\widetilde{\psi}_{t,k}(\widetilde{\beta}_{t}^{(2)})\equiv 0
. Consider the following decomposition:

\displaystyle\left|\psi_{t}(\widetilde{\beta}_{t}^{(2)})\right|\lesssim

\displaystyle\ \left|\psi_{t}(\widetilde{\beta}_{t}^{(2)})-\bar{\psi}_{t,k}(\widetilde{\beta}_{t}^{(2)})\right|+\left|\bar{\psi}_{t,k}(\beta^{*}_{t})-\psi_{t}(\beta^{*}_{t})\right|

\displaystyle+\left|\left\{\bar{\psi}_{t,k}(\widetilde{\beta}_{t}^{(2)})-\widetilde{\psi}_{t,k}^{(2)}(\widetilde{\beta}_{t}^{(2)})\right\}-\left\{\bar{\psi}_{t,k}(\beta^{*}_{t})-\widetilde{\psi}_{t,k}^{(2)}(\beta^{*}_{t})\right\}\right|

\displaystyle+\left|\widetilde{\psi}_{t,k}^{(2)}(\widetilde{\beta}_{t}^{(2)})-\widetilde{\psi}_{t,k}^{(2)}(\beta^{*}_{t})\right|

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\ \sup_{\beta\in{\mathcal{B}}}\left|\psi_{t}(\beta)-\bar{\psi}_{t,k}(\beta)\right|

(S8.10)

\displaystyle+\sup_{\beta\in{\mathcal{B}}}\left|\left\{\bar{\psi}_{t,k}(\beta)-\widetilde{\psi}_{t,k}^{(2)}(\beta)\right\}-\left\{\bar{\psi}_{t,k}(\beta^{*}_{t})-\widetilde{\psi}_{t,k}^{(2)}(\beta^{*}_{t})\right\}\right|

(S8.11)

\displaystyle+\left|\widetilde{\psi}_{t,k}^{(2)}(\beta^{*}_{t})\right|.

(S8.12)

Here, the first term (
S8.10
) contributes to the bias, the third term (
S8.12
) captures the variance, and the second term (
S8.11
) is a
U
-process indexed by
\beta
.

First, we analyze the term (
S8.10
).
Applying Lemmas
17
and
18
,
we obtain the following bias decomposition:

\displaystyle\ \psi_{t}(\beta)-\bar{\psi}_{t,k}(\beta)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\ {\mathbb{E}}[\xi(X,T)b_{\beta}(X,T)|T=t]\mathsf{p}_{T}(t)-{\mathbb{E}}\left[K_{t,h}(T_{1})\widehat{\xi}(X_{1},T_{1})\{\Gamma(Y_{1},T_{1},\beta)-\widehat{b}_{\beta_{\mathsf{init}}}(X_{1},T_{1})\}\right]

\displaystyle+{\mathbb{E}}\left[\widehat{b}_{\beta_{\mathsf{init}}}(X_{1},T_{2})K_{t,h}(T_{2})\right]

\displaystyle-{\mathbb{E}}\left[\{K_{t,h}(T_{1})\widehat{\xi}(X_{1},T_{1})-K_{t,h}(T_{3})\}\bar{{\mathsf{z}}}_{k_{x}}^{\top}(X_{1})\widetilde{\Omega}_{k_{x}}^{-1}\bar{{\mathsf{z}}}_{k_{x}}(X_{2})\{\Gamma(Y_{2},T_{2},\beta)-\widehat{b}_{\beta_{\mathsf{init}}}(X_{2},T_{2})\}K_{t,h}(T_{2})\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\ {\mathbb{E}}\left[K_{t,h}(T)\left\{\xi(X,t)-\widehat{\xi}(X,t)\right\}\left\{b_{\beta}(X,t)-\widehat{b}_{\beta_{\mathsf{init}}}(X,t)\right\}\right]

\displaystyle-\int_{x\in{\mathcal{X}}}\Pi_{g}[\widehat{\xi}(\cdot,t)-\xi(\cdot,t)|\bar{{\mathsf{z}}}_{k_{x}}](x)\times\Pi_{g}[b_{\beta}(\cdot,t)-\widehat{b}_{\beta_{\mathsf{init}}}(\cdot,t)|\bar{{\mathsf{z}}}_{k_{x}}](x)g(x){\mathrm{d}}x+O(h^{\alpha_{1}\wedge\alpha_{2}})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\ \int_{x\in{\mathcal{X}}}\left\{\xi(x,t)-\widehat{\xi}(x,t)\right\}\left\{b_{\beta}(x,t)-\widehat{b}_{\beta_{\mathsf{init}}}(x,t)\right\}g(x){\mathrm{d}}x

\displaystyle-\int_{x\in{\mathcal{X}}}\Pi_{g}[\widehat{\xi}(\cdot,t)-\xi(\cdot,t)|\bar{{\mathsf{z}}}_{k_{x}}](x)\times\Pi_{g}[b_{\beta}(\cdot,t)-\widehat{b}_{\beta_{\mathsf{init}}}(\cdot,t)|\bar{{\mathsf{z}}}_{k_{x}}](x)g(x){\mathrm{d}}x+O(h^{\alpha_{1}\wedge\alpha_{2}})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\int_{x\in{\mathcal{X}}}\Pi_{g}^{\perp}[b_{\beta}(\cdot,t)-\widehat{b}_{\beta_{\mathsf{init}}}(\cdot,t)\mid\bar{{\mathsf{z}}}_{k_{x}}](x)\times\Pi_{g}^{\perp}[\xi(\cdot,t)-\widehat{\xi}(\cdot,t)\mid\bar{{\mathsf{z}}}_{k_{x}}](x)g(x){\mathrm{d}}x+O(h^{\alpha_{1}\wedge\alpha_{2}}),

(S8.13)

where
\Pi_{g}[f\mid\bar{{\mathsf{z}}}_{k_{x}}](\cdot)\coloneqq\int_{{\mathcal{X}}}f(x)\bar{{\mathsf{z}}}_{k_{x}}(x)^{\top}g(x){\mathrm{d}}x\,\widetilde{\Omega}_{k_{x}}^{-1}\,\bar{{\mathsf{z}}}_{k_{x}}(\cdot)
denotes the projection (with respect to the weight
g
) of
f
onto the linear span of
\bar{{\mathsf{z}}}_{k_{x}}
and
\Pi^{\perp}_{g}[f\mid\bar{{\mathsf{z}}}_{k_{x}}](\cdot)\coloneqq f(\cdot)-\Pi_{g}[f\mid\bar{{\mathsf{z}}}_{k_{x}}](\cdot)
denotes the orthocomplement of this projection.
This shows that the leading bias is determined by the product of the projection errors of
b_{\beta}(\cdot,t)-\widehat{b}_{\beta_{\mathsf{init}}}(\cdot,t)
and
\xi(\cdot,t)-\widehat{\xi}(\cdot,t)
onto the span of
\bar{{\mathsf{z}}}_{k_{x}}
, plus a kernel approximation error of order
O(h^{\alpha_{1}\wedge\alpha_{2}})
.
Thus, we conclude that

\displaystyle\eqref{eq:kernel-consist-1}\lesssim

\displaystyle\sup_{\beta\in{\mathcal{B}}}\left\|\Pi_{g}^{\perp}[b_{\beta}(\cdot,t)-\widehat{b}_{\beta_{\mathsf{init}}}(\cdot,t)\mid\bar{{\mathsf{z}}}_{k_{x}}]\right\|_{g,2}\cdot\left\|\Pi_{g}^{\perp}[\xi(\cdot,t)-\widehat{\xi}(\cdot,t)\mid\bar{{\mathsf{z}}}_{k_{x}}]\right\|_{g,2}+O(h^{\alpha_{1}\wedge\alpha_{2}}).

(S8.14)

Here,
\|\cdot\|_{g,2}
denotes the norm
\|f\|_{g,2}=\{\int_{{\mathcal{X}}}f(x)^{2}g(x){\mathrm{d}}x\}^{1/2}
for any function
f(x)
.

Next, we analyze the
U
-process term (
S8.11
). Since
{\mathcal{B}}
is compact, we may write
\sup_{\beta\in{\mathcal{B}}}
as
\sup_{|\beta-\beta^{*}_{t}|\leq c}
for some constant
c
sufficiently large to cover
{\mathcal{B}}
.
Applying Lemma
23
with
\epsilon=c
, we obtain

\displaystyle\eqref{eq:kernel-consist-2}=O_{\mathbb{P}}\left(\frac{\sqrt{k}}{nh}+\frac{1}{\sqrt{nh}}\right)\cdot\log n.

(S8.15)

Last, for the term (
S8.12
), applying the decomposition in (
S8.13
) together with Lemma
22
, we have

\displaystyle|\widetilde{\psi}_{t,k}^{(2)}(\beta^{\ast}_{t})|=

\displaystyle\ O\left(\left\|\Pi_{g}^{\perp}[b_{\beta_{t}^{\ast}}(\cdot,t)-\widehat{b}_{\beta_{\mathsf{init}}}(\cdot,t)\mid\bar{{\mathsf{z}}}_{k_{x}}]\right\|_{g,2}\cdot\left\|\Pi_{g}^{\perp}[\xi(\cdot,t)-\widehat{\xi}(\cdot,t)\mid\bar{{\mathsf{z}}}_{k_{x}}]\right\|_{g,2}\right)

\displaystyle+O(h^{\alpha_{1}\wedge\alpha_{2}})+O_{\mathbb{P}}\left(\frac{\sqrt{k_{x}}}{nh}+\frac{1}{\sqrt{nh}}\right).

Combining the bounds for terms (
S8.10
)–(
S8.12
), we have, with probability approaching one,

\displaystyle\left|\psi_{t}(\widetilde{\beta}_{t}^{(2)})\right|\lesssim

\displaystyle\ \eqref{eq:kernel-consist-1}+\eqref{eq:kernel-consist-2}+\eqref{eq:kernel-consist-3}\lesssim\ \widetilde{r}_{n,\mathsf{np}}.

This establishes the consistency of
\widetilde{\beta}_{t}^{(2)}
.

Building on this consistency result, we now restrict our analysis to the region
{|\beta-\beta_{t}^{*}|\lesssim\widetilde{r}_{n,\mathsf{np}}}
.
This refinement allows us to improve upon the initial rate
\widetilde{r}_{n,\mathsf{np}}
and obtain the final convergence rate stated in Theorem
6
. Repeating the decomposition from the consistency argument, but now over the restricted region, we have

\displaystyle\left|\psi_{t}(\widetilde{\beta}_{t}^{(2)})\right|\lesssim

\displaystyle\ \sup_{|\beta-\beta^{\ast}_{t}|\lesssim\,\widetilde{r}_{n,\mathsf{np}}}\left|\psi_{t}(\beta)-\bar{\psi}_{t,k}(\beta)\right|+\left|\widetilde{\psi}_{t,k}^{(2)}(\beta^{*}_{t})\right|

\displaystyle+\sup_{|\beta-\beta^{\ast}_{t}|\lesssim\,\widetilde{r}_{n,\mathsf{np}}}\left|\left\{\bar{\psi}_{t,k}(\beta)-\widetilde{\psi}_{t,k}^{(2)}(\beta)\right\}-\left\{\bar{\psi}_{t,k}(\beta^{*}_{t})-\widetilde{\psi}_{t,k}^{(2)}(\beta^{*}_{t})\right\}\right|.

The only change from the earlier argument is that the supremum is now taken over a shrinking neighborhood of
\beta_{t}^{*}
. Applying the bias decomposition (
S8.13
), Lemma
23
with
\epsilon=\widetilde{r}_{n,\mathsf{np}}
, and Lemma
22
, we obtain, with probability approaching one,

\displaystyle\left|\psi_{t}(\widetilde{\beta}^{(2)}_{t})\right|\lesssim

\displaystyle\ \sup_{|\beta-\beta^{*}_{t}|\lesssim\,\widetilde{r}_{n,\mathsf{np}}}\left\|\Pi_{g}^{\perp}[b_{\beta}(\cdot,t)-\widehat{b}_{\beta_{\mathsf{init}}}(\cdot,t)\mid\bar{{\mathsf{z}}}_{k_{x}}]\right\|_{g,2}\cdot\left\|\Pi_{g}^{\perp}[\xi(\cdot,t)-\widehat{\xi}(\cdot,t)\mid\bar{{\mathsf{z}}}_{k_{x}}]\right\|_{g,2}

\displaystyle+h^{\alpha_{1}\wedge\alpha_{2}}+\frac{\sqrt{k_{x}}}{nh}+\frac{1}{\sqrt{nh}}+\left(\frac{\sqrt{k_{x}}}{nh}+\frac{1}{\sqrt{nh}}\right)\cdot\{(\widetilde{r}_{n,\mathsf{np}})^{\alpha_{0}}\vee\sqrt{h}\}\cdot\log n.

Given the additional conditions
h(\log n)^{2}\to 0
and
(\widetilde{r}_{n,\mathsf{np}})^{\alpha_{0}}\log n\to 0
, this further simplifies to

\displaystyle\left|\psi_{t}(\widetilde{\beta}^{(2)}_{t})\right|\lesssim

\displaystyle\ \frac{\sqrt{k_{x}}}{nh}+\frac{1}{\sqrt{nh}}+h^{\alpha_{1}\wedge\alpha_{2}}

\displaystyle+\sup_{|\beta-\beta^{*}_{t}|\lesssim\,\widetilde{r}_{n,\mathsf{np}}}\left\|\Pi_{g}^{\perp}[b_{\beta}(\cdot,t)-\widehat{b}_{\beta_{\mathsf{init}}}(\cdot,t)\mid\bar{{\mathsf{z}}}_{k_{x}}]\right\|_{g,2}\cdot\left\|\Pi_{g}^{\perp}[\xi(\cdot,t)-\widehat{\xi}(\cdot,t)\mid\bar{{\mathsf{z}}}_{k_{x}}]\right\|_{g,2}

with probability approaching one. Combining this with (
S8.9
) yields the final convergence rate for
\widetilde{\beta}_{t}^{(2)}
, thus completing the proof.

∎

S8.2
Technical Lemmas for Proving Theorem
6

Lemma 17
.

Under Assumptions
13
and
15
, we have

\displaystyle\ {\mathbb{E}}[\xi(X,T)b_{\beta}(X,T)|T=t]\mathsf{p}_{T}(t)={\mathbb{E}}[\xi(X,t)b_{\beta}(X,t)K_{t,h}(T)]+O(h^{\alpha_{1}\wedge\alpha_{2}}),

\displaystyle\ {\mathbb{E}}\left[K_{t,h}(T_{1})\widehat{\xi}(X_{1},T_{1})\{\Gamma(Y_{1},T_{1},\beta)-\widehat{b}_{\beta_{\mathsf{init}}}(X_{1},T_{1})\}+\widehat{b}_{\beta_{\mathsf{init}}}(X_{1},T_{2})K_{t,h}(T_{2})\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\ {\mathbb{E}}\left[K_{t,h}(T)\widehat{\xi}(X,t)\{b_{\beta}(X,t)-\widehat{b}_{\beta_{\mathsf{init}}}(X,t)\}+\xi(X,t)\widehat{b}_{\beta_{\mathsf{init}}}(X,t)K_{t,h}(T)\right]+O(h^{\alpha_{1}\wedge\alpha_{2}}).

Proof.

Using a similar argument for proving (
S8.4
), we have

\displaystyle\sup_{x\in{\mathcal{X}}}\left|\int_{u\in{\mathcal{T}}}K_{t,h}(u)\mathsf{p}_{T|X}(u|x){\mathrm{d}}u-\mathsf{p}_{T|X}(t|x)\right|=O(h^{\alpha_{1}\wedge\alpha_{2}}).

Thus

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}[\xi(X,t)b_{\beta}(X,t)K_{t,h}(T)]=

\displaystyle\int_{x\in{\mathcal{X}}}\xi(x,t)b_{\beta}(x,t)\int_{u\in{\mathcal{T}}}K_{t,h}(u)\mathsf{p}_{T|X}(u|x){\mathrm{d}}u\mathsf{p}_{X}(x){\mathrm{d}}x

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\int_{x\in{\mathcal{X}}}\xi(x,t)b_{\beta}(x,t)\mathsf{p}_{T|X}(t|x)\mathsf{p}_{X}(x){\mathrm{d}}x+O(h^{\alpha_{1}\wedge\alpha_{2}})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\int_{x\in{\mathcal{X}}}b_{\beta}(x,t)\mathsf{p}_{T}(t)\mathsf{p}_{X}(x){\mathrm{d}}x+O(h^{\alpha_{1}\wedge\alpha_{2}})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\ {\mathbb{E}}[\xi(X,T)b_{\beta}(X,T)|T=t]\mathsf{p}_{T}(t)+O(h^{\alpha_{1}\wedge\alpha_{2}}).

Hence, we obtain the first result.

Using the definition of
\xi(x,t)
, we have

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}\left[K_{t,h}(T_{1})\widehat{\xi}(X_{1},T_{1})\{\Gamma(Y_{1},T_{1},\beta)-\widehat{b}_{\beta_{\mathsf{init}}}(X_{1},T_{1})\}+\widehat{b}_{\beta_{\mathsf{init}}}(X_{1},T_{2})K_{t,h}(T_{2})\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\int_{x\in{\mathcal{X}}}\int_{u\in{\mathcal{T}}}K_{t,h}(u)\left\{\widehat{\xi}(x,u)(b_{\beta}(x,u)-\widehat{b}_{\beta_{\mathsf{init}}}(x,u))+\xi(x,u)\widehat{b}_{\beta_{\mathsf{init}}}(x,u)\right\}\mathsf{p}_{T|X}(u|x){\mathrm{d}}u\mathsf{p}_{X}(x){\mathrm{d}}x

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\int_{x\in{\mathcal{X}}}\int_{u\in{\mathcal{T}}}K_{t,h}(u)\left\{\widehat{\xi}(x,t)(b_{\beta}(x,t)-\widehat{b}_{\beta_{\mathsf{init}}}(x,t))+\xi(x,t)\widehat{b}_{\beta_{\mathsf{init}}}(x,t)\right\}\mathsf{p}_{T|X}(u|x){\mathrm{d}}u\mathsf{p}_{X}(x){\mathrm{d}}x+O(h^{\alpha_{1}\wedge\alpha_{2}})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\ {\mathbb{E}}\left[K_{t,h}(T)\left\{\widehat{\xi}(X,t)(b_{\beta}(X,t)-\widehat{b}_{\beta_{\mathsf{init}}}(X,t))+\xi(X,t)\widehat{b}_{\beta_{\mathsf{init}}}(X,t)\right\}\right]+O(h^{\alpha_{1}\wedge\alpha_{2}}),

where the second equality uses a similar argument for proving (
S8.4
).
∎

Lemma 18
.

Under Assumptions
6
–
8
,
13
and
15
, we have

\displaystyle\ \bigg|{\mathbb{E}}\left[\{\widehat{\xi}(X,T)-\xi(X,T)\}\bar{{\mathsf{z}}}_{k_{x}}(X)^{\top}K_{t,h}(T)\right]\widetilde{\Omega}_{k_{x}}^{-1}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\bar{{\mathsf{z}}}_{k_{x}}(X)\{\Gamma(Y,T,\beta)-\widehat{b}_{\beta_{\mathsf{init}}}(X,T)\}K_{t,h}(T)\right]

\displaystyle-\int_{x\in{\mathcal{X}}}\Pi_{g}[\widehat{\xi}(\cdot,t)-\xi(\cdot,t)|\bar{{\mathsf{z}}}_{k_{x}}](x)\times\Pi_{g}[b_{\beta}(\cdot,t)-\widehat{b}_{\beta_{\mathsf{init}}}(\cdot,t)|\bar{{\mathsf{z}}}_{k_{x}}](x)g(x){\mathrm{d}}x\bigg|=O(h^{\alpha_{1}\wedge\alpha_{2}}).

Proof.

By the definition of
\xi(X,T)
,

\displaystyle\ {\mathbb{E}}\left[\{K_{t,h}(T_{1})\widehat{\xi}(X_{1},T_{1})-K_{t,h}(T_{3})\}\bar{{\mathsf{z}}}_{k_{x}}^{\top}(X_{1})\widetilde{\Omega}_{k_{x}}^{-1}\bar{{\mathsf{z}}}_{k_{x}}(X_{2})\{\Gamma(Y_{2},T_{2},\beta)-\widehat{b}_{\beta_{\mathsf{init}}}(X_{2},T_{2})\}K_{t,h}(T_{2})\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\ {\mathbb{E}}\left[\{\widehat{\xi}(X,T)-\xi(X,T)\}\bar{{\mathsf{z}}}_{k_{x}}(X)^{\top}K_{t,h}(T)\right]\widetilde{\Omega}_{k_{x}}^{-1}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\bar{{\mathsf{z}}}_{k_{x}}(X)\{b_{\beta}(X,T)-\widehat{b}_{\beta_{\mathsf{init}}}(X,T)\}K_{t,h}(T)\right].

Recalling the definitions of
\Delta_{1}(X)
and
\Delta_{2}(X)
in (
S8.2
) and (
S8.3
), we have

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}\left[\{b_{\beta}(X,T)-\widehat{b}_{\beta_{\mathsf{init}}}(X,T)-b_{\beta}(X,t)+\widehat{b}_{\beta_{\mathsf{init}}}(X,t)\}K_{t,h}(T)\bar{{\mathsf{z}}}_{k_{x}}(X)\right]={\mathbb{E}}[\Delta_{2}(X)\bar{{\mathsf{z}}}_{k_{x}}(X)^{\top}]

and

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}[\{\widehat{\xi}(X,T)-\xi(X,T)-\widehat{\xi}(X,t)+\xi(X,t)\}\bar{{\mathsf{z}}}_{k_{x}}(X)^{\top}K_{t,h}(T)]={\mathbb{E}}[\Delta_{1}(X)\bar{{\mathsf{z}}}_{k_{x}}(X)^{\top}].

Then, we obtain

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}[\{\widehat{\xi}(X,T)-\xi(X,T)\}\bar{{\mathsf{z}}}_{k_{x}}(X)^{\top}K_{t,h}(T)]\widetilde{\Omega}_{k_{x}}^{-1}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\bar{{\mathsf{z}}}_{k_{x}}(X)\{\Gamma(Y,T,\beta)-\widehat{b}_{\beta_{\mathsf{init}}}(X,T)\}K_{t,h}(T)\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\left({\mathbb{E}}[\{\widehat{\xi}(X,t)-\xi(X,t)\}\bar{{\mathsf{z}}}_{k_{x}}(X)^{\top}K_{t,h}(T)]+{\mathbb{E}}[\Delta_{1}(X)\bar{{\mathsf{z}}}_{k_{x}}(X)^{\top}]\right)\times\widetilde{\Omega}_{k_{x}}^{-1}

\displaystyle\times\left({\mathbb{E}}[\{b_{\beta}(X,t)-\widehat{b}_{\beta_{\mathsf{init}}}(X,t)\}\bar{{\mathsf{z}}}_{k_{x}}(X)K_{t,h}(T)]+{\mathbb{E}}[\Delta_{2}(X)\bar{{\mathsf{z}}}_{k_{x}}(X)]\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\left[\int_{x\in{\mathcal{X}}}\{\widehat{\xi}(x,t)-\xi(x,t)\}\bar{{\mathsf{z}}}_{k_{x}}(x)^{\top}g(x){\mathrm{d}}x+\int_{x\in{\mathcal{X}}}\Delta_{1}(x)\lambda(x)\bar{{\mathsf{z}}}_{k_{x}}(x)^{\top}g(x){\mathrm{d}}x\right]

\displaystyle\times\widetilde{\Omega}_{k_{x}}^{-1}\times\left[\int_{x\in{\mathcal{X}}}\{b_{\beta}(x,t)-\widehat{b}_{\beta_{\mathsf{init}}}(x,t)\}\bar{{\mathsf{z}}}_{k_{x}}(x)g(x){\mathrm{d}}x+\int_{x\in{\mathcal{X}}}\Delta_{2}(x)\lambda(x)\bar{{\mathsf{z}}}_{k_{x}}(x)^{\top}g(x){\mathrm{d}}x\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\int_{x\in{\mathcal{X}}}\left\{\Pi_{g}[\widehat{\xi}(\cdot,t)-\xi(\cdot,t)|\bar{{\mathsf{z}}}_{k_{x}}](x)+\Pi_{g}[\Delta_{1}\lambda|\bar{{\mathsf{z}}}_{k_{x}}](x)\right\}

\displaystyle\times\left\{\Pi_{g}[b_{\beta}(\cdot,t)-\widehat{b}_{\beta_{\mathsf{init}}}(\cdot,t)|\bar{{\mathsf{z}}}_{k_{x}}](x)+\Pi_{g}[\Delta_{2}\lambda|\bar{{\mathsf{z}}}_{k_{x}}](x)\right\}g(x){\mathrm{d}}x.

where the second equality holds by using (
S8.5
) and (
S8.6
).
Hence

\displaystyle\ \bigg|{\mathbb{E}}[\{\widehat{\xi}(X,T)-\xi(X,T)\}\bar{{\mathsf{z}}}_{k_{x}}(X)^{\top}K_{t,h}(T)]\widetilde{\Omega}_{k_{x}}^{-1}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\bar{{\mathsf{z}}}_{k_{x}}(X)\{\Gamma(Y,T,\beta)-\widehat{b}_{\beta_{\mathsf{init}}}(X,T)\}K_{t,h}(T)\right]

\displaystyle-\int_{x\in{\mathcal{X}}}\Pi_{g}[\widehat{\xi}(\cdot,t)-\xi(\cdot,t)|\bar{{\mathsf{z}}}_{k_{x}}](x)\times\Pi_{g}[b_{\beta}(\cdot,t)-\widehat{b}_{\beta_{\mathsf{init}}}(\cdot,t)|\bar{{\mathsf{z}}}_{k_{x}}](x)g(x){\mathrm{d}}x\bigg|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\ \bigg|\int_{x\in{\mathcal{X}}}\Pi_{g}[\widehat{\xi}(\cdot,t)-\xi(\cdot,t)|\bar{{\mathsf{z}}}_{k_{x}}](x)\times\Pi_{g}[\Delta_{2}\lambda|\bar{{\mathsf{z}}}_{k_{x}}](x)g(x){\mathrm{d}}x\bigg|

\displaystyle+\bigg|\int_{x\in{\mathcal{X}}}\Pi_{g}[\Delta_{1}\lambda|\bar{{\mathsf{z}}}_{k_{x}}](x)\times\Pi_{g}[b_{\beta}(\cdot,t)-\widehat{b}_{\beta_{\mathsf{init}}}(\cdot,t)|\bar{{\mathsf{z}}}_{k_{x}}](x)g(x){\mathrm{d}}x\bigg|

\displaystyle+\bigg|\int_{x\in{\mathcal{X}}}\Pi_{g}[\Delta_{1}\lambda|\bar{{\mathsf{z}}}_{k_{x}}](x)\times\Pi_{g}[\Delta_{2}\lambda|\bar{{\mathsf{z}}}_{k_{x}}](x)g(x){\mathrm{d}}x\bigg|

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\ \|\Pi_{g}[\widehat{\xi}(\cdot,t)-\xi(\cdot,t)|\bar{{\mathsf{z}}}_{k_{x}}]\|_{g,2}\cdot\|\Pi_{g}[\Delta_{2}\lambda|\bar{{\mathsf{z}}}_{k_{x}}]\|_{g,2}

\displaystyle+\left\|\Pi_{g}[\Delta_{1}\lambda|\bar{{\mathsf{z}}}_{k_{x}}]\right\|_{g,2}\cdot\left\|\Pi_{g}[b_{\beta}(\cdot,t)-\widehat{b}_{\beta_{\mathsf{init}}}(\cdot,t)|\bar{{\mathsf{z}}}_{k_{x}}]\right\|_{g,2}

\displaystyle+\left\|\Pi_{g}[\Delta_{1}\lambda|\bar{{\mathsf{z}}}_{k_{x}}]\right\|_{g,2}\cdot\left\|\Pi_{g}[\Delta_{2}\lambda|\bar{{\mathsf{z}}}_{k_{x}}]\right\|_{g,2}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\ \|\widehat{\xi}(X,t)-\xi(X,t)\|_{g,2}\cdot\|\Delta_{2}(X)\lambda(X)\|_{g,2}

\displaystyle+\left\|\Delta_{1}(X)\lambda(X)\right\|_{g,2}\cdot\left\|b_{\beta}(X,t)-\widehat{b}_{\beta_{\mathsf{init}}}(X,t)\right\|_{g,2}

\displaystyle+\left\|\Delta_{1}(X)\lambda(X)\right\|_{g,2}\cdot\left\|\Delta_{2}(X)\lambda(X)\right\|_{g,2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\ O(h^{\alpha_{1}\wedge\alpha_{2}}),

(S8.16)

where the last equality holds by using Assumptions
6
,
15
, and (
S8.4
).
∎

Lemma 19
.

Under Assumptions
6
,
7
,
13
, and
15
,
we have

\displaystyle\ {\mathbb{E}}\bigg[\sup_{|\beta_{1}-\beta_{2}|\lesssim\epsilon}\left|\{\Gamma(Y,T,\beta_{1})-\Gamma(Y,T,\beta_{2})\}K_{t,h}\left(T\right)\right|^{2}\bigg]=O\left(\frac{\epsilon^{2\alpha_{0}}\vee h}{h}\right),

\displaystyle a^{\top}{\mathbb{E}}\left[K_{t,h}(T)^{2}\{\widehat{\xi}(X,T)-1\}^{2}\xi(X,T)\bar{{\mathsf{z}}}_{k_{x}}(X)\bar{{\mathsf{z}}}_{k_{x}}(X)^{\top}\right]a\lesssim\frac{1}{h}a^{\top}\widetilde{\Omega}_{k_{x}}a,

where
a\in{\mathbb{R}}^{k_{x}}
is a unit vector.

Proof.

By Taylor expansion,
Assumptions
6
and (
S8.8
), we have

\displaystyle\ {\mathbb{E}}\bigg[\sup_{|\beta_{1}-\beta_{2}|\lesssim\epsilon}\left|\{\Gamma(Y,T,\beta_{1})-\Gamma(Y,T,\beta_{2})\}K_{t,h}\left(T\right)\right|^{2}\bigg]

(S8.17)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\ \frac{1}{h^{2}}\int_{u\in{\mathcal{T}}}{\mathbb{E}}\bigg[\sup_{|\beta_{1}-\beta_{2}|\lesssim\epsilon}\left|\widehat{\xi}(X,T)\{\Gamma(Y,T,\beta_{1})-\Gamma(Y,T,\beta_{2})\}\right|^{2}\mid T=u\bigg]\left|K\left(\frac{u-t}{h}\right)\right|^{2}\mathsf{p}_{T}(u){\mathrm{d}}u

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\ \frac{1}{h}\int_{u\in{\mathcal{T}}}{\mathbb{E}}\bigg[\sup_{|\beta_{1}-\beta_{2}|\leq\epsilon}\left|\widehat{\xi}(X,T)\{\Gamma(Y,T,\beta_{1})-\Gamma(Y,T,\beta_{2})\}\right|^{2}\mid T=t+hu\bigg]\left|K\left(u\right)\right|^{2}\mathsf{p}_{T}(t+hu){\mathrm{d}}u

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\ \frac{1}{h}{\mathbb{E}}\bigg[\sup_{|\beta_{1}-\beta_{2}|\lesssim\epsilon}\left|\widehat{\xi}(X,T)\{\Gamma(Y,T,\beta_{1})-\Gamma(Y,T,\beta_{2})\}\right|^{2}\mid T=t\bigg]\mathsf{p}_{T}(T=t)\cdot\kappa_{0,2}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{h}\int_{u\in{\mathcal{T}}}\nabla_{t}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left.\sup_{|\beta_{1}-\beta_{2}|\lesssim\epsilon}\left|\widehat{\xi}(X,T)\{\Gamma(Y,T,\beta_{1})-\Gamma(Y,T,\beta_{2})\}\right|^{2}\right|T=t+\tau hu\right]huK(u)^{2}{\mathrm{d}}u

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\ \frac{1}{h}\left\{{\mathbb{E}}\left[\sup_{|\beta_{1}-\beta_{2}|\lesssim\epsilon}\left|\Gamma(Y,T,\beta_{1})-\Gamma(Y,T,\beta_{2})\right|^{2}\mid T=t\right]\mathsf{p}_{T}(T=t)\cdot\kappa_{0,2}+O(h)\right\}=O\left(\frac{\epsilon^{2\alpha_{0}}\vee h}{h}\right),

where
\tau\in(0,1)
and
\kappa_{0,2}=\int K\left(u\right)^{2}{\mathrm{d}}u
. Thus, we obtain the first result. Similarly, we have
{\mathbb{E}}[K_{t,h}(T)^{2}]=O\left({1}/{h}\right)
. Then, we obtain

\displaystyle\ a^{\top}{\mathbb{E}}\left[K_{t,h}(T)^{2}\{\widehat{\xi}(X,T)-1\}^{2}\xi(X,T)\bar{{\mathsf{z}}}_{k_{x}}(X)\bar{{\mathsf{z}}}_{k_{x}}(X)^{\top}\right]a

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\ a^{\top}{\mathbb{E}}\left[K_{t,h}(T)^{2}\xi(X,T)\bar{{\mathsf{z}}}_{k_{x}}(X)\bar{{\mathsf{z}}}_{k_{x}}(X)^{\top}\right]a

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\ {\mathbb{E}}\left[K_{t,h}(T)^{2}\right]\cdot a^{\top}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\bar{{\mathsf{z}}}_{k_{x}}(X)\bar{{\mathsf{z}}}_{k_{x}}(X)^{\top}\right]a

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\ {\mathbb{E}}\left[K_{t,h}(T)^{2}\right]\cdot a^{\top}\int_{x\in{\mathcal{X}}}\bar{{\mathsf{z}}}_{k_{x}}(x)\bar{{\mathsf{z}}}_{k_{x}}(x)^{\top}g(x)\cdot\frac{\mathsf{p}_{X}(x)}{g(x)}{\mathrm{d}}xa

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\ \sup_{x\in{\mathcal{X}}}\left|\frac{\mathsf{p}_{X}(x)}{g(x)}\right|\cdot{\mathbb{E}}\{K_{t,h}(T)^{2}\}\cdot a^{\top}\widetilde{\Omega}_{k_{x}}\,a\lesssim\frac{1}{h}a^{\top}\widetilde{\Omega}_{k_{x}}\,a,

where the first inequality holds since we have
\|\widehat{\xi}\|\lesssim 1
; the first equality holds by the definition of
\xi(x,t)
.
∎

Lemma 20
.

Under Assumptions
6
–
8
,
13
, and
15
, we have the following results:

\displaystyle\mathrm{(i)}

\displaystyle\sup\limits_{|\beta-\beta^{\ast}_{t}|\lesssim\epsilon}{\mathbb{E}}\big[\big|{\mathbb{E}}[\{\widehat{\xi}(X,T)-\xi(X,T)\}K_{t,h}(T)\bar{{\mathsf{z}}}_{k_{x}}(X)^{\top}]\widetilde{\Omega}_{k_{x}}^{-1}\bar{{\mathsf{z}}}_{k_{x}}(X)K_{t,h}(T)(\Gamma_{\beta}-\Gamma_{\beta^{\ast}_{t}})(Y,T)\big|^{2}\big]

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\epsilon^{2\alpha_{0}}\vee h}{h};

\displaystyle\mathrm{(ii)}

\displaystyle\sup\limits_{|\beta-\beta^{\ast}_{t}|\lesssim\epsilon}{\mathbb{E}}\big[\big|K_{t,h}(T){\mathbb{E}}[\bar{{\mathsf{z}}}_{k_{x}}(X)^{\top}]\widetilde{\Omega}_{k_{x}}^{-1}{\mathbb{E}}[\bar{{\mathsf{z}}}_{k_{x}}(X)K_{t,h}(T)\{b_{\beta}(X,T)-b_{\beta^{\ast}_{t}}(X,T)\}]\big|^{2}\big]

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\epsilon^{2\alpha_{0}}\vee h^{2(\alpha_{1}\wedge\alpha_{2})}}{h};

\displaystyle\mathrm{(iii)}

\displaystyle\sup\limits_{|\beta-\beta^{\ast}_{t}|\lesssim\epsilon}{\mathbb{E}}\big[\big|K_{t,h}(T)\widehat{\xi}(X,T)\bar{{\mathsf{z}}}_{k_{x}}(X)^{\top}\widetilde{\Omega}_{k_{x}}^{-1}{\mathbb{E}}[\bar{{\mathsf{z}}}_{k_{x}}(X)K_{t,h}(T)\{b_{\beta}(X,T)-b_{\beta^{\ast}_{t}}(X,T)\}]\big|^{2}\big]

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\epsilon^{2\alpha_{0}}\vee h^{2(\alpha_{1}\wedge\alpha_{2})}}{h};

\displaystyle\mathrm{(iv)}

\displaystyle\sup\limits_{|\beta-\beta^{\ast}_{t}|\lesssim\epsilon}{\mathbb{E}}\big[\big|{\mathbb{E}}[K_{t,h}(T)]\bar{{\mathsf{z}}}_{k_{x}}(X)^{\top}\widetilde{\Omega}_{k_{x}}^{-1}{\mathbb{E}}[\bar{{\mathsf{z}}}_{k_{x}}(X)K_{t,h}(T)\{b_{\beta}(X,T)-b_{\beta^{\ast}_{t}}(X,T)\}]\big|^{2}\big]

\displaystyle\lesssim\epsilon^{2\alpha_{0}}\vee h^{2(\alpha_{1}\wedge\alpha_{2})}.

Proof.

To aid the exposition, we define the following quantities:

\displaystyle\Delta_{3}(x)\coloneqq

\displaystyle\ \int_{u\in{\mathcal{T}}}\{\widehat{\xi}(x,u)-\xi(x,u)\}K_{t,h}(u)\mathsf{p}_{T|X}(u|x){\mathrm{d}}u,

(S8.18)

\displaystyle\Delta_{4}(x)\coloneqq

\displaystyle\ \int_{u\in{\mathcal{T}}}\{b_{\beta}(x,u)-b_{\beta^{\ast}_{t}}(x,u)\}K_{t,h}(u)\mathsf{p}_{T|X}(u|x){\mathrm{d}}u-\{b_{\beta}(x,t)-b_{\beta^{\ast}_{t}}(x,t)\}\mathsf{p}_{T|X}(t|x)

(S8.19)

\displaystyle\Delta_{5}(x)\coloneqq

\displaystyle\ \Delta_{4}(x)+\{b_{\beta}(x,t)-b_{\beta^{\ast}_{t}}(x,t)\}\mathsf{p}_{T|X}(t|x)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\ \int_{u\in{\mathcal{T}}}\{b_{\beta}(x,u)-b_{\beta^{\ast}_{t}}(x,u)\}K_{t,h}(u)\mathsf{p}_{T|X}(u|x){\mathrm{d}}u

(S8.20)

Note that both
\Delta_{4}(x)
and
\Delta_{5}(x)
depend on
\beta
, but, for notational convenience, we suppress this argument. Assumption
15
states that the functions
b_{\beta}
share the same degree of smoothness for every
\beta
, we can repeat the argument used to establish (
S8.4
) and obtain the following results:

\displaystyle\sup_{x\in{\mathcal{X}}}|\Delta_{3}(x)|\lesssim 1,\ \sup_{\beta}\sup_{x\in{\mathcal{X}}}|\Delta_{4}(x)|\lesssim h^{\alpha_{1}\wedge\alpha_{2}},\ \text{and}\ \sup_{\beta}\sup_{x\in{\mathcal{X}}}|\Delta_{5}(x)|\lesssim 1.

(S8.21)

First, we have

\displaystyle\ \sup_{|\beta-\beta^{\ast}_{t}|\lesssim\epsilon}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left|\Pi_{g}[\Delta_{5}(\cdot)\lambda(\cdot)|\bar{{\mathsf{z}}}_{k_{x}}](X)\right|^{2}\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\ \sup_{|\beta-\beta^{\ast}_{t}|\lesssim\epsilon}\int_{x\in{\mathcal{X}}}\left|\Pi_{g}[\Delta_{5}(\cdot)\lambda(\cdot)|\bar{{\mathsf{z}}}_{k_{x}}](x)\right|^{2}\lambda(x)g(x){\mathrm{d}}x

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\ \sup_{|\beta-\beta^{\ast}_{t}|\lesssim\epsilon}\int_{x\in{\mathcal{X}}}\left|\Delta_{5}(x)\lambda(x)\right|^{2}g(x){\mathrm{d}}x

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\ \sup_{|\beta-\beta^{\ast}_{t}|\lesssim\epsilon}\sup_{x\in{\mathcal{X}}}|\Delta_{4}(x)|^{2}+\sup_{|\beta-\beta^{\ast}_{t}|\lesssim\epsilon}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\xi(X,T)\big\{b_{\beta}(X,T)-b_{\beta^{\ast}_{t}}(X,T)\big\}^{2}|T=t\right]

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\ h^{2(\alpha_{1}\wedge\alpha_{2})}+{\mathbb{E}}\left[\sup_{|\beta-\beta^{\ast}_{t}|\lesssim\epsilon}[\Gamma(Y,T,\beta)-\Gamma(Y,T,\beta^{\ast}_{t})]^{2}|T=t\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\ O\left(\epsilon^{2\alpha_{0}}\vee h^{2(\alpha_{1}\wedge\alpha_{2})}\right),

(S8.22)

where the first equality holds by (
S8.5
); the first inequality by (
S8.4
) and the properties of projections; the second inequality holds by definition of
\xi(x,t)
and
\sup_{x\in{\mathcal{X}}}|\lambda(x)|\lesssim 1
; the third inequality holds by (
S8.21
) and Jensen’s inequality; the last equality by (
S8.8
).

Recalling the definition of
\Delta_{3}(x)
in (
S8.18
) and using (
S8.5
),
we have

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}[\{\widehat{\xi}(X,T)-\xi(X,T)\}\bar{{\mathsf{z}}}_{k_{x}}(X)K_{t,h}(T)]={\mathbb{E}}[\Delta_{3}(X)\bar{{\mathsf{z}}}_{k_{x}}(X)]=\int_{x\in{\mathcal{X}}}\Delta_{3}(x)\lambda(x)\bar{{\mathsf{z}}}_{k_{x}}(x)g(x){\mathrm{d}}x,

(S8.23)

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}[\{b_{\beta}(X,T)-b_{\beta^{\ast}_{t}}(X,T)\}\bar{{\mathsf{z}}}_{k_{x}}(X)K_{t,h}(T)]={\mathbb{E}}[\Delta_{5}(X)\bar{{\mathsf{z}}}_{k_{x}}(x)]=\int_{x\in{\mathcal{X}}}\Delta_{5}(x)\lambda(x)\bar{{\mathsf{z}}}_{k_{x}}(x)g(x){\mathrm{d}}x.

(S8.24)

Then, we have

\displaystyle\ {\mathbb{E}}\left[\sup_{|\beta-\beta^{\ast}_{t}|\lesssim\epsilon}\left|{\mathbb{E}}[\{\widehat{\xi}(X,T)-\xi(X,T)\}K_{t,h}(T)\bar{{\mathsf{z}}}_{k_{x}}(X)^{\top}]\widetilde{\Omega}_{k_{x}}^{-1}\bar{{\mathsf{z}}}_{k_{x}}(X)K_{t,h}(T)[\Gamma(Y,T,\beta)-\Gamma(Y,T,\beta^{\ast}_{t})]\right|^{2}\right]

(S8.25)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\ {\mathbb{E}}\left[\sup_{|\beta-\beta^{\ast}_{t}|\lesssim\epsilon}\left|\int_{x\in{\mathcal{X}}}\Delta_{3}(x)\lambda(x)\bar{{\mathsf{z}}}_{k_{x}}(x)^{\top}g(x){\mathrm{d}}x\widetilde{\Omega}_{k_{x}}^{-1}\bar{{\mathsf{z}}}_{k_{x}}(X)K_{t,h}(T)[\Gamma(Y,T,\beta)-\Gamma(Y,T,\beta^{\ast}_{t})]\right|^{2}\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\ {\mathbb{E}}\left[\sup_{|\beta-\beta^{\ast}_{t}|\lesssim\epsilon}\left|\Pi_{g}\left[\Delta_{3}(X)\lambda(X)\big|\bar{{\mathsf{z}}}_{k_{x}}(X)\right]K_{t,h}(T)[\Gamma(Y,T,\beta)-\Gamma(Y,T,\beta^{\ast}_{t})]\right|^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\ \sup_{x\in{\mathcal{X}}}\left|\Pi_{g}\left[\Delta_{3}(\cdot)\lambda(\cdot)\big|\bar{{\mathsf{z}}}_{k_{x}}\right](x)\right|^{2}\cdot{\mathbb{E}}\left[\sup_{|\beta-\beta^{\ast}_{t}|\lesssim\epsilon}\left|K_{t,h}(T)[\Gamma(Y,T,\beta)-\Gamma(Y,T,\beta^{\ast}_{t})]\right|^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\ \sup_{x\in{\mathcal{X}}}\left|\Delta_{3}(x)\lambda(x)\right|^{2}\cdot{\mathbb{E}}\left[\sup_{|\beta-\beta^{\ast}_{t}|\lesssim\epsilon}\left|K_{t,h}(T)[\Gamma(Y,T,\beta)-\Gamma(Y,T,\beta^{\ast}_{t})]\right|^{2}\right]=O\left(\frac{\epsilon^{2\alpha_{0}}\vee h}{h}\right),

where the last inequality holds by (
S8.7
) and the last equality holds by Lemma
19
and (
S8.4
). Hence, we obtain the result
(i)
.

For the result
(ii)
,

\displaystyle\ \sup_{|\beta-\beta^{\ast}_{t}|\lesssim\epsilon}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left|K_{t,h}(T){\mathbb{E}}[\bar{{\mathsf{z}}}_{k_{x}}(X)^{\top}]\widetilde{\Omega}_{k_{x}}^{-1}{\mathbb{E}}[\bar{{\mathsf{z}}}_{k_{x}}(X)K_{t,h}(T)\{b_{\beta}(X,T)-b_{\beta^{\ast}_{t}}(X,T)\}]\right|^{2}\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\ {\mathbb{E}}\left[\left|K_{t,h}(T)\right|^{2}\right]\cdot\sup_{|\beta-\beta^{\ast}_{t}|\lesssim\epsilon}\left|{\mathbb{E}}[\bar{{\mathsf{z}}}_{k_{x}}(X)^{\top}]\widetilde{\Omega}_{k_{x}}^{-1}{\mathbb{E}}[\bar{{\mathsf{z}}}_{k_{x}}(X)K_{t,h}(T)\{b_{\beta}(X,T)-b_{\beta^{\ast}_{t}}(X,T)\}]\right|^{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\ {\mathbb{E}}\left[\left|K_{t,h}(T)\right|^{2}\right]\cdot\sup_{|\beta-\beta^{\ast}_{t}|\lesssim\epsilon}\left|{\mathbb{E}}[\bar{{\mathsf{z}}}_{k_{x}}(X)^{\top}]\widetilde{\Omega}_{k_{x}}^{-1}\int_{x\in{\mathcal{X}}}\bar{{\mathsf{z}}}_{k_{x}}(x)\Delta_{5}(x)\lambda(x)g(x){\mathrm{d}}x\right|^{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\ {\mathbb{E}}\left[\left|K_{t,h}(T)\right|^{2}\right]\cdot\sup_{|\beta-\beta^{\ast}_{t}|\lesssim\epsilon}\left|{\mathbb{E}}\left[\Pi_{g}[\Delta_{5}(\cdot)\lambda(\cdot)|\bar{{\mathsf{z}}}_{k_{x}}](X)\right]\right|^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\ {\mathbb{E}}\left[\left|K_{t,h}(T)\right|^{2}\right]\cdot\sup_{|\beta-\beta^{\ast}_{t}|\lesssim\epsilon}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left|\Pi_{g}[\Delta_{5}(\cdot)\lambda(\cdot)|\bar{{\mathsf{z}}}_{k_{x}}](X)\right|^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\ \frac{\epsilon^{2\alpha_{0}}\vee h^{2(\alpha_{1}\wedge\alpha_{2})}}{h},

where the second equality uses (
S8.24
) and the last inequality holds by (
S8.2
).

For the result
(iii)
,

\displaystyle\ \sup_{|\beta-\beta^{\ast}_{t}|\lesssim\epsilon}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left|K_{t,h}(T)\widehat{\xi}(X,T)\bar{{\mathsf{z}}}_{k_{x}}(X)^{\top}\widetilde{\Omega}_{k_{x}}^{-1}{\mathbb{E}}[\bar{{\mathsf{z}}}_{k_{x}}(X)K_{t,h}(T)\{b_{\beta}(X,T)-b_{\beta^{\ast}_{t}}(X,T)\}]\right|^{2}\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\ \sup_{|\beta-\beta^{\ast}_{t}|\lesssim\epsilon}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left|K_{t,h}(T)\widehat{\xi}(X,T)\bar{{\mathsf{z}}}_{k_{x}}(X)^{\top}\widetilde{\Omega}_{k_{x}}^{-1}\int_{x\in{\mathcal{X}}}\bar{{\mathsf{z}}}_{k_{x}}(x)\Delta_{5}(x)\lambda(x)g(x){\mathrm{d}}x\right|^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\ \sup_{|\beta-\beta^{\ast}_{t}|\lesssim\epsilon}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left|K_{t,h}(T)\Pi_{g}[\Delta_{5}(\cdot)\lambda(\cdot)|\bar{{\mathsf{z}}}_{k_{x}}](X)\right|^{2}\xi(X,T)\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\ {\mathbb{E}}\left[\left|K_{t,h}(T)\right|^{2}\right]\cdot\sup_{|\beta-\beta^{\ast}_{t}|\lesssim\epsilon}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left|\Pi_{g}[\Delta_{5}(\cdot)\lambda(\cdot)|\bar{{\mathsf{z}}}_{k_{x}}](X)\right|^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\ \frac{\epsilon^{2\alpha_{0}}\vee h^{2(\alpha_{1}\wedge\alpha_{2})}}{h},

where the first inequality holds since
\xi(x,t)
and
\widehat{\xi}(x,t)
are uniformly bounded, and the last inequality holds by (
S8.2
).

Similarly, for the result
(iv)
, we have

\displaystyle\sup_{|\beta-\beta^{\ast}_{t}|\lesssim\epsilon}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left|{\mathbb{E}}[K_{t,h}(T)]\bar{{\mathsf{z}}}_{k_{x}}(X)^{\top}\widetilde{\Omega}_{k_{x}}^{-1}{\mathbb{E}}[\bar{{\mathsf{z}}}_{k_{x}}(X)K_{t,h}(T)\{b_{\beta}(X,T)-b_{\beta^{\ast}_{t}}(X,T)\}]\right|^{2}\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\left|{\mathbb{E}}[K_{t,h}(T)]\right|^{2}\sup_{|\beta-\beta^{\ast}_{t}|\lesssim\epsilon}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left|\bar{{\mathsf{z}}}_{k_{x}}(X)^{\top}\widetilde{\Omega}_{k_{x}}^{-1}\int_{x\in{\mathcal{X}}}\bar{{\mathsf{z}}}_{k_{x}}(x)\Delta_{5}(x)\lambda(x)g(x){\mathrm{d}}x\right|^{2}\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\left|{\mathbb{E}}[K_{t,h}(T)]\right|^{2}\sup_{|\beta-\beta^{\ast}_{t}|\lesssim\epsilon}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left|\Pi_{g}[\Delta_{5}(\cdot)\lambda(\cdot)|\bar{{\mathsf{z}}}_{k_{x}}](X)\right|^{2}\right]\lesssim\epsilon^{2\alpha_{0}}\vee h^{2(\alpha_{1}\wedge\alpha_{2})},

where the last inequality uses
{\mathbb{E}}[K_{t,h}(T)]\lesssim 1
and (
S8.2
).
∎

Lemma 21
.

Under Assumptions
6
–
8
,
13
, and
15
, for any
h\in{\mathcal{H}}_{4,n}
, we have

\displaystyle\sup_{\beta\in{\mathcal{B}}}\sup_{o\in{\mathcal{O}}}|\mathbb{P}^{2}h(o;\beta)|\lesssim\frac{1}{h}.

Proof.

For any
h\in{\mathcal{H}}_{4,n}
, the explicit expression of
\mathbb{P}^{2}h
is given by

\displaystyle\ \mathbb{P}^{2}h(O;\beta)

(S8.26)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\ \frac{1}{3}\{K_{t,h}(T)\widehat{\xi}(X,T)-{\mathbb{E}}[K_{t,h}(T)]\}\bar{{\mathsf{z}}}_{k_{x}}(X)^{\top}\widetilde{\Omega}_{k_{x}}^{-1}{\mathbb{E}}[\bar{{\mathsf{z}}}_{k_{x}}(X)K_{t,h}(T)\{b_{\beta}(X,T)-b_{\beta^{\ast}_{t}}(X,T)\}]

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{3}{\mathbb{E}}[\{\widehat{\xi}(X,T)-\xi(X,T)\}K_{t,h}(T)\bar{{\mathsf{z}}}_{k_{x}}(X)^{\top}]\widetilde{\Omega}_{k_{x}}^{-1}\bar{{\mathsf{z}}}_{k_{x}}(X)K_{t,h}(T)[\Gamma(Y,T,\beta)-\Gamma(Y,T,\beta^{\ast}_{t})]

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{3}K_{t,h}(T){\mathbb{E}}[\bar{{\mathsf{z}}}_{k_{x}}(X)^{\top}]\widetilde{\Omega}_{k_{x}}^{-1}{\mathbb{E}}[\bar{{\mathsf{z}}}_{k_{x}}(X)K_{t,h}(T)\{b_{\beta}(X,T)-b_{\beta^{\ast}_{t}}(X,T)\}].

Recalling the definitions of
\Delta_{3}(x)
and
\Delta_{4}(x)
in (
S8.18
) and (
S8.20
), for any
h\in{\mathcal{H}}_{4,n}
, we have:

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}^{2}h(O;\beta)=

\displaystyle\ \frac{1}{3}\{K_{t,h}(T)\widehat{\xi}(X,T)-{\mathbb{E}}[K_{t,h}(T)]\}\Pi_{g}[\Delta_{5}(\cdot)\lambda(\cdot)|\bar{{\mathsf{z}}}_{k_{x}}](X)

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{3}\Pi_{g}\left[\Delta_{3}(\cdot)\lambda(\cdot)\big|\bar{{\mathsf{z}}}_{k_{x}}\right](X)K_{t,h}(T)[\Gamma(Y,T,\beta)-\Gamma(Y,T,\beta^{\ast}_{t})]

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{3}K_{t,h}(T){\mathbb{E}}\left[\Pi_{g}[\Delta_{5}(\cdot)\lambda(\cdot)|\bar{{\mathsf{z}}}_{k_{x}}](X)\right].

By (
S8.7
), we have

\displaystyle\sup_{x\in{\mathcal{X}}}\left|\Pi_{g}[\Delta_{3}(\cdot)\lambda(\cdot)|\bar{{\mathsf{z}}}_{k_{x}}](x)\right|\lesssim

\displaystyle\sup_{x\in{\mathcal{X}}}\left|\Delta_{3}(x)\lambda(x)\right|\lesssim 1,

\displaystyle\text{and}\ \sup_{x\in{\mathcal{X}}}\left|\Pi_{g}[\Delta_{5}(\cdot)\lambda(\cdot)|\bar{{\mathsf{z}}}_{k_{x}}](x)\right|\lesssim

\displaystyle\sup_{x\in{\mathcal{X}}}\left|\Delta_{5}(x)\lambda(x)\right|\lesssim 1.

Since
\|\widehat{\xi}(\cdot,\cdot)\|_{\infty}\lesssim 1
,
\|\Gamma(\cdot,\cdot,\beta)\|_{\infty}\lesssim 1
, and
\|K_{t,h}(\cdot)\|_{\infty}\lesssim 1/h
, it follows that

\displaystyle\sup_{\beta\in{\mathcal{B}}}\sup_{o\in{\mathcal{O}}}|\pi_{1}h(o;\beta)|\lesssim\frac{1}{h},

which completes the proof.
∎

Lemma 22
.

Suppose that Assumptions
6
–
8
and
13
hold and
k_{x},n\to\infty
with
k_{x}=o(n^{2})
. Then, for a given
\beta
, conditioning on the nuisance sample,

\displaystyle\mathrm{var}\left(\widetilde{\psi}_{t,k}^{(2)}(\beta)\right)=O\left(\frac{1}{nh}+\frac{k_{x}}{n^{2}h^{2}}\right).

Proof.

First, we have:

\displaystyle\mathrm{var}\left(\widetilde{\psi}_{t,k}^{(2)}(\beta)\right)\lesssim\mathrm{var}\left({\mathbb{U}}_{n,2}\widehat{\Upsilon}_{\beta}\right)+\mathrm{var}\left({\mathbb{U}}_{n,3}\left(\mathsf{IF}_{\mathrm{B}_{\psi,t,k}}^{(2)}(\beta)\right)\right),

where

\displaystyle\widehat{\Upsilon}_{\beta}(O_{1},O_{2})\coloneqq K_{t,h}(T_{1})\widehat{\xi}(X_{1},T_{1})\{\Gamma_{\beta}-\widehat{b}_{\beta_{\mathsf{init}}}(X_{1},T_{1})\}+\widehat{b}_{\beta_{\mathsf{init}}}(X_{1},T_{2})K_{t,h}(T_{2})\text{ and }

\displaystyle\mathsf{IF}_{\mathrm{B}_{\psi,t,k}}^{(2)}(\beta)\coloneqq\{K_{t,h}(T_{1})\widehat{\xi}(X_{1},T_{1})-K_{t,h}(T_{3})\}\bar{{\mathsf{z}}}_{k_{x}}^{\top}(X_{1})\widetilde{\Omega}_{k_{x}}^{-1}\bar{{\mathsf{z}}}_{k_{x}}(X_{2})\{\Gamma(Y_{2},T_{2},\beta)-\widehat{b}_{\beta_{\mathsf{init}}}(X_{2},T_{2})\}K_{t,h}(T_{2}).

Using Lemma 15 in
(
15
)
and a Taylor expansion argument similar to that used in the proof of Lemma
19
, we obtain

\displaystyle\ \mathrm{var}\left({\mathbb{U}}_{n,2}\widehat{\Upsilon}_{\beta}\right)=\mathrm{var}\left[{\mathbb{U}}_{n,2}\left\{S_{2}\left(\widehat{\Upsilon}_{\beta}\right)\right\}\right]

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\ \frac{1}{n}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left\{{\mathbb{E}}\left[S_{2}\left(\widehat{\Upsilon}_{\beta}\right)\mid O_{1}\right]\right\}^{2}\right]+\frac{1}{n^{2}}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left\{{\mathbb{E}}\left[S_{2}\left(\widehat{\Upsilon}_{\beta}\right)\mid O_{1},O_{2}\right]\right\}^{2}\right]\lesssim\frac{1}{nh},

where
S_{2}
is the symmetrization operator defined in Section
S6.1
.
Similarly, for
\mathrm{var}\left\{{\mathbb{U}}_{n,3}\left(\mathsf{IF}_{\mathrm{B}_{\psi,t,k}}^{(2)}(\beta)\right)\right\}
, we have

\displaystyle\ \mathrm{var}\left({\mathbb{U}}_{n,3}\left(\mathsf{IF}_{\mathrm{B}_{\psi,t,k}}^{(2)}(\beta)\right)\right)=\mathrm{var}\left[{\mathbb{U}}_{n,3}\left\{S_{3}\left(\mathsf{IF}_{\mathrm{B}_{\psi,t,k}}^{(2)}(\beta)\right)\right\}\right]

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\ \sum_{j=1}^{3}\frac{1}{n^{j}}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left\{{\mathbb{E}}\left[S_{3}\left(\mathsf{IF}_{\mathrm{B}_{\psi,t,k}}^{(2)}(\beta)\right)\mid O_{1},\cdots,O_{j}\right]\right\}^{2}\right]\eqqcolon\sum_{j=1}^{3}\zeta_{j}.

Following (
S8.26
) and Lemma
20
, we have

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left\{{\mathbb{E}}[S(\mathsf{IF}_{\mathrm{B}_{\psi,t,k}}^{(2)}(\beta))\mid O_{1}]\right\}^{2}\right]\lesssim\frac{1}{h},\text{ and hence }\zeta_{1}\lesssim\frac{1}{nh}.

For
\zeta_{3}
, following the proof of (
S8.31
), we conclude

\displaystyle\zeta_{3}\lesssim\frac{1}{n^{3}}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left(\mathsf{IF}_{\mathrm{B}_{\psi,t,k}}^{(2)}(\beta)\right)^{2}\right]\lesssim\frac{k_{x}}{n^{3}h^{2}}.

For
\zeta_{2}
, by Jensen’s inequality, we obtain:

\displaystyle\zeta_{2}\lesssim\frac{1}{n^{2}}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left(\mathsf{IF}_{\mathrm{B}_{\psi,t,k}}^{(2)}(\beta)\right)^{2}\right]\lesssim\frac{k_{x}}{n^{2}h^{2}}.

Combining all the analysis above, we obtain

\displaystyle\mathrm{var}\left({\mathbb{U}}_{n,3}\left(\mathsf{IF}_{\mathrm{B}_{\psi,t,k}}^{(2)}(\beta)\right)\right)\lesssim\sum_{j=1}^{3}\zeta_{j}\lesssim\frac{1}{nh}+\frac{k_{x}}{n^{2}h^{2}},

which completes the proof.
∎

Lemma 23
.

Under Assumptions
6
–
8
,
13
, and
15
, for any
\epsilon>0
,

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}\bigg[\sup_{|\beta-\beta^{\ast}_{t}|\lesssim\epsilon}\left|\left\{\bar{\psi}_{t,k}(\beta)-\widetilde{\psi}_{t,k}^{(2)}(\beta)\right\}-\left\{\bar{\psi}_{t,k}(\beta^{*}_{t})-\widetilde{\psi}_{t,k}^{(2)}(\beta^{*}_{t})\right\}\right|\bigg]\lesssim\left(\frac{\sqrt{k}}{nh}+\frac{1}{\sqrt{nh}}\right)\cdot(\epsilon^{\alpha_{0}}\vee\sqrt{h})\cdot\log n.

Proof.

We begin by decomposing the supremum as follows:

\displaystyle\sup_{|\beta-\beta^{\ast}_{t}|\lesssim\epsilon}\left|\bar{\psi}_{t,k}(\beta)-\widetilde{\psi}_{t,k}^{(2)}(\beta)-\bar{\psi}_{t,k}(\beta^{\ast}_{t})+\widetilde{\psi}_{t,k}^{(2)}(\beta^{\ast}_{t})\right|

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sup_{|\beta-\beta^{\ast}_{t}|\lesssim\epsilon}\left|\bar{\psi}_{t,k}(\beta)-\widehat{\psi}^{(1)}_{t}(\beta)-{\mathbb{U}}_{n,3}[\mathsf{IF}_{\mathrm{B}_{\psi,t,k}}^{(2)}(\beta)]-\bar{\psi}_{t,k}(\beta^{\ast}_{t})+\widehat{\psi}^{(1)}_{t}(\beta_{t}^{*})+{\mathbb{U}}_{n,3}[\mathsf{IF}_{\mathrm{B}_{\psi,t,k}}^{(2)}(\beta_{t}^{*})]\right|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\ \sup_{|\beta-\beta^{\ast}_{t}|\lesssim\epsilon}\left|{\mathbb{U}}_{n,3}\left\{\mathsf{IF}_{\mathrm{B}_{\psi,t,k}}^{(2)}(\beta)-\mathsf{IF}_{\mathrm{B}_{\psi,t,k}}^{(2)}(\beta_{t}^{*})-{\mathbb{E}}\left(\mathsf{IF}_{\mathrm{B}_{\psi,t,k}}^{(2)}(\beta)-\mathsf{IF}_{\mathrm{B}_{\psi,t,k}}^{(2)}(\beta_{t}^{*})\right)\right\}\right|

(S8.27)

\displaystyle+\sup_{|\beta-\beta^{\ast}_{t}|\lesssim\epsilon}\left|\widehat{\psi}^{(1)}_{t}(\beta)-\widehat{\psi}^{(1)}_{t}(\beta_{t}^{*})-{\mathbb{E}}\left[\widehat{\psi}^{(1)}_{t}(\beta)-\widehat{\psi}^{(1)}_{t}(\beta_{t}^{*})\right]\right|.

(S8.28)

We first analyze the term (
S8.28
). Consider the function class

\displaystyle{\mathcal{H}}_{3}\coloneqq\left\{h(O,\beta)=K_{t,h}(T)\widehat{\xi}(X,T)[\Gamma(Y,T,\beta)-\Gamma(Y,T,\beta^{*}_{t})]:\beta\in{\mathcal{B}},|\beta-\beta^{\ast}_{t}|\lesssim\epsilon\right\}.

with envelope function
\mathrm{H}_{3}(O)\coloneqq\sup_{|\beta-\beta^{\ast}_{t}|\lesssim\epsilon}|K_{t,h}(T)\widehat{\xi}(X,T)[\Gamma(Y,T,\beta)-\Gamma(Y,T,\beta^{*}_{t})]|
. By an argument similar to that for
{\mathcal{H}}_{1,\delta}
(see (
S7.20
)), we find that
{\mathcal{H}}_{3}
is also of VC type with bounded characteristics. Since
\|\widehat{\xi}(\cdot,\cdot)\|_{\infty}\lesssim 1
, Lemma
19
gives

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}[|\mathrm{H}_{3}(O)|^{2}]=

\displaystyle\ {\mathbb{E}}\left[\sup_{|\beta-\beta^{\ast}_{t}|\lesssim\epsilon}\left|\widehat{\xi}(X,T)\{\Gamma(Y,T,\beta)-\Gamma(Y,T,\beta^{*}_{t})\}K_{t,h}\left(T\right)\right|^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\ {\mathbb{E}}\left[\sup_{|\beta-\beta^{\ast}_{t}|\lesssim\epsilon}\left|\{\Gamma(Y,T,\beta)-\Gamma(Y,T,\beta^{*}_{t})\}K_{t,h}\left(T\right)\right|^{2}\right]=O\left(\frac{\epsilon^{2\alpha_{0}}\vee h}{h}\right).

(S8.29)

Thus,
\|\mathrm{H}_{3}\|_{\mathbb{P},2}\lesssim(\epsilon^{\alpha_{0}}\vee\sqrt{h})/\sqrt{h}
. Applying Theorem 2.14.1 of
van der Vaart and Wellner 2023
to
{\mathcal{H}}_{3}
yields

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}\left[\big|\eqref{eq:Uprocess-kernel-order1}\big|\right]={\mathbb{E}}\left[\left\|{\mathbb{U}}_{n}(h)\right\|_{{\mathcal{H}}_{3}}\right]

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{J_{1}(1,{\mathcal{H}}_{3},\mathrm{H}_{3})\cdot\|\mathrm{H}_{3}\|_{\mathbb{P},2}}{\sqrt{n}}\lesssim\frac{\epsilon^{\alpha_{0}}\vee\sqrt{h}}{\sqrt{nh}}.

(S8.30)

We now turn to the term (
S8.27
). Consider the function class

\displaystyle{\mathcal{H}}_{4,n}\coloneqq

\displaystyle\left\{h(O_{1},O_{2},O_{3},\beta)=\mathsf{IF}_{\mathrm{B}_{\psi,t,k}}^{(2)}(\beta)-\mathsf{IF}_{\mathrm{B}_{\psi,t,k}}^{(2)}(\beta_{t}^{*}):\beta\in{\mathcal{B}},|\beta-\beta^{\ast}_{t}|\lesssim\epsilon\right\}.

Similarly, we can show that
{\mathcal{H}}_{4,n}
is also of VC type with bounded characteristics and envelope

\displaystyle\mathrm{H}_{4,n}(O_{1},O_{2},O_{3})\coloneqq

\displaystyle\ |\{K_{t,h}(T_{1})\widehat{\xi}(X_{1},T_{1})-K_{t,h}(T_{3})\}\bar{{\mathsf{z}}}_{k_{x}}(X_{1})^{\top}\widetilde{\Omega}_{k_{x}}^{-1}\bar{{\mathsf{z}}}_{k_{x}}(X_{2})K_{t,h}(T_{2})|

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\times\sup_{|\beta-\beta^{\ast}_{t}|\lesssim\epsilon}|\Gamma(Y_{2},T_{2},\beta)-\Gamma(Y_{2},T_{2},\beta^{*}_{t})|.

Under Assumptions
6
,
8
and
13
, we have
\|\mathrm{H}_{4,n}(\cdot,\cdot,\cdot)\|_{\infty}\lesssim h^{2}\zeta(k_{x})^{2}\lesssim k_{x}h^{2}
. In addition,

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}[|\mathrm{H}_{4,n}(O_{1},O_{2},O_{3})|^{2}]

(S8.31)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\ {\mathbb{E}}\bigg[\sup_{|\beta-\beta^{\ast}_{t}|\lesssim\epsilon}|\Gamma(Y,T,\beta)-\Gamma(Y,T,\beta^{*}_{t})|^{2}K_{t,h}(T)^{2}\bar{{\mathsf{z}}}_{k_{x}}(X)^{\top}\widetilde{\Omega}_{k_{x}}^{-1}

\displaystyle\times{\mathbb{E}}\left[K_{t,h}(T)^{2}\{\widehat{\xi}(X,T)-1\}^{2}\xi(X,T)\bar{{\mathsf{z}}}_{k_{x}}(X)\bar{{\mathsf{z}}}_{k_{x}}(X)^{\top}\right]\widetilde{\Omega}_{k_{x}}^{-1}\bar{{\mathsf{z}}}_{k_{x}}(X)\bigg]

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\ \frac{1}{h}{\mathbb{E}}\bigg[\sup_{|\beta-\beta^{\ast}_{t}|\lesssim\epsilon}|\Gamma(Y,T,\beta)-\Gamma(Y,T,\beta^{*}_{t})|^{2}K_{t,h}(T)^{2}\bar{{\mathsf{z}}}_{k_{x}}(X)^{\top}\widetilde{\Omega}_{k_{x}}^{-1}\cdot\widetilde{\Omega}_{k_{x}}\cdot\widetilde{\Omega}_{k_{x}}^{-1}\bar{{\mathsf{z}}}_{k_{x}}(X)\bigg]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\ \frac{1}{h}{\mathbb{E}}\bigg[\sup_{|\beta-\beta^{\ast}_{t}|\lesssim\epsilon}|\Gamma(Y,T,\beta)-\Gamma(Y,T,\beta^{*}_{t})|^{2}\big|K_{t,h}(T)^{2}\bar{{\mathsf{z}}}_{k_{x}}(X)^{\top}\widetilde{\Omega}_{k_{x}}^{-1}\bar{{\mathsf{z}}}_{k_{x}}(X)\big|\bigg]

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\ \frac{\zeta(k_{x})^{2}}{h}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\sup_{|\beta-\beta^{\ast}_{t}|\lesssim\epsilon}|\Gamma(Y,T,\beta)-\Gamma(Y,T,\beta^{*}_{t})|^{2}\cdot\big|K_{t,h}(T)\big|^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\ \frac{k_{x}\cdot(\epsilon^{2\alpha_{0}}\vee h)}{h^{2}},

where the inequalities follow from Lemma
19
and Assumption
8
. Thus,
\|\mathrm{H}_{4,n}\|_{\mathbb{P}^{3},2}\lesssim\sqrt{k_{x}}(\epsilon^{\alpha_{0}}\vee\sqrt{h})/h
. Further, by Lemma
20
, we have
\sup_{h\in{\mathcal{H}}_{4,n}}\|\mathbb{P}^{2}h\|_{\mathbb{P},2}\lesssim(\epsilon^{\alpha_{0}}\vee\sqrt{h})/\sqrt{h}
and by Lemma
21
,
\sup_{h\in{\mathcal{H}}_{4,n}}\|\mathbb{P}^{2}h(\cdot)\|_{\infty}\lesssim 1/h
.
Thus, applying Lemma
10
yields

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}\left[\big|\eqref{eq:Uprocess-kernel-order1}\big|\right]={\mathbb{E}}\left[\left\|{\mathbb{U}}_{n,3}(h)\right\|_{{\mathcal{H}}_{4,n}}\right]\lesssim\frac{\sqrt{k_{x}}\cdot(\epsilon^{\alpha_{0}}\vee\sqrt{h})\cdot\log n}{nh}+\frac{(\epsilon^{\alpha_{0}}\vee\sqrt{h})\cdot\sqrt{\log n}}{\sqrt{nh}}.

(S8.32)

Therefore, combining (
S8.30
) and (
S8.32
) completes the proof.
∎

Appendix S9
Supplementary Information on the Numerical Experiments

S9.1
Supplementary simulation results

In this section, we collect supplementary information on the simulation study conducted in Section
5.1
. In particular, we display the normal-QQ plots of our proposed higher-order estimators over various settings in the simulation (i.e. varying the sample size
n
and the smoothness parameter
s
): see Figure
5
for
\beta_{0}^{*}
and Figure
6
for
\beta_{1}^{*}
below for Case 1 and Figure
7
for
\beta_{0}^{*}
and Figure
8
for
\beta_{1}^{*}
for Case 2.

It is quite clear that the higher-order estimator proposed here for QTE with binary treatment is asymptotically normally distributed across different sample sizes n ∈{250, 500, 750, 1000, 1500, 2000, 2500, 3000} and different smoothness levels of the nuisance parameters with
s=0.25,0.4,0.6
.

The GOE estimator
(
2
)
is obtained by solving (
6
) with
\widehat{\xi}
estimated through the following quadratic programming problem:

\displaystyle\min_{\bm{\xi}}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\frac{(\xi_{i}-1)^{2}}{2}\text{ s.t. }\ \frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\xi_{i}\bar{\phi}_{k}(X_{i},T_{i})=\frac{1}{n(n-1)}\sum_{1\leq i\neq j\leq n}\bar{\phi}_{k}(X_{i},T_{j}).

Here,
\bm{\xi}=(\xi_{1},\ldots,\xi_{n})
with
\xi_{i}\equiv\xi(X_{i},T_{i})
, and
\bar{\phi}_{k}
is a power series basis of length
k
. The parameter
k
is selected by minimizing a cross-validation criterion.

Sensitivity analyses of our proposed higher-order estimators are also conducted, specifically on the choice of
k
and on the initial estimator
\bm{\beta}_{\mathsf{init}}
.

Figure 5
:
Normal-QQ plots for the first coordinate of
\widehat{\bm{\beta}}^{(2)}
across all simulation settings in Case 1, based on 1000 Monte Carlo replications.

Figure 6
:
Normal-QQ plots for the second coordinate of
\widehat{\bm{\beta}}^{(2)}
across all simulation settings in Case 1, based on 1000 Monte Carlo replications.

:
Normal-QQ plots for the first coordinate of
\widehat{\bm{\beta}}^{(2)}
across all simulation settings in Case 2, based on 1000 Monte Carlo replications.

:
Normal-QQ plots for the second coordinate of
\widehat{\bm{\beta}}^{(2)}
across all simulation settings in Case 2, based on 1000 Monte Carlo replications.

:
Sensitivity of the higher-order estimator for the QTE,
\beta_{1}^{*}-\beta_{0}^{*}
, to the choice of spline basis dimension
k
. The horizontal axis reports the knot factor
\lambda
, where the number of interior knots for each continuous covariate is set to
\lceil\lambda n/100\rceil
. The three panels report the empirical mean squared error, variance, and absolute bias across 1000 Monte Carlo replications for Cases 1 and 2 with
n=2000
and
s=0.25
.

Figure 10
:
Sensitivity of the higher-order estimator for the QTE,
\beta_{1}^{*}-\beta_{0}^{*}
, to perturbations in the initial estimator. The horizontal axis reports the perturbation level
\delta
, where the perturbed initial estimator is defined by
\bm{\beta}_{\mathsf{init}}^{(\delta)}=\bm{\beta}_{\mathsf{init}}+\delta(-1,1)^{\top}
. The boxplots display the resulting QTE estimation errors across 1000 Monte Carlo replications for Cases 1 and 2 with
n=2000
and
s=0.25
.

Table 4
:
Sensitivity of the HOE estimates of the QTE of 401(k) eligibility (in thousand dollars) to deterministic perturbations of the initial estimator. The perturbation follows the same scheme as in the simulation study, namely
\bm{\beta}_{\mathsf{init}}^{(\delta)}=\bm{\beta}_{\mathsf{init}}+\delta(-1,1)^{\top}
, where
\delta
denotes the perturbation level. Here
\tau\in\{25\%,50\%,75\%\}
denotes the quantile level of the QTE.
